if not (superAdmins[localPlayer.Name] or admins[localPlayer.Name] or allowedUsers[localPlayer.Name]) then
	return
else
	function getUserRole(key)
		if superAdmins[key] then
			return "superadmin"
		elseif admins[key] then
			return "admin"
		else
			if allowedUsers[key] then
				return "allowed"
			end

			return "user"
		end
	end

	role = getUserRole(localPlayer.Name)
	spin = false
	spinTarget = nil
	loopKill = false
	loopKillTarget = nil
	frozen = false
	rbxGeneral = textChatService.TextChannels:WaitForChild("RBXGeneral")

	function addOwnerTag(p1)
		if not p1.Character then
			return
		else
			local waitForChild = p1.Character:WaitForChild("Head", 5)

			if not waitForChild then
				return
			elseif waitForChild:FindFirstChild("OwnerTag") then
				return
			else
				local ownerTag = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
				ownerTag.Name = "OwnerTag"
				ownerTag.Size = UDim2.new(0, 200, 0, 50)
				ownerTag.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)
				ownerTag.AlwaysOnTop = true
				ownerTag.Parent = waitForChild

				local textLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
				textLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
				textLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
				textLabel.Text = "Owner (BloodyV2)"
				textLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0)
				textLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.5
				textLabel.TextScaled = true
				textLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
				textLabel.Parent = ownerTag

				return
			end
		end
	end

	if role == "admin" or role == "allowed" then
		for index, value in ipairs(players:GetPlayers()) do
			local character87 = value

			if superAdmins[character87.Name] then
				if character87.Character then
					addOwnerTag(character87)
				end

				character87.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
					wait(0.5)
					addOwnerTag(character87)
				end)
			end
		end

		players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(p2)
			if superAdmins[p2.Name] then
				p2.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
					wait(0.5)
					addOwnerTag(p2)
				end)
			end
		end)
	end

	function sendToChat(p3)
		rbxGeneral:SendAsync(p3)
	end

	function canControlRole(p4, p5)
		if p4 == "superadmin" then
			return p5 == "admin" or p5 == "allowed" or p5 == "user"
		end

		if p4 == "admin" then
			return p5 == "allowed" or p5 == "user"
		end

		return false
	end

	function toggleBlock(p6, p7)
		local character2 = p6.Character
		local rootPart88 = character2 and character2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

		if not rootPart88 then
			return
		end

		if p7 then
			if not rootPart88:FindFirstChild("Block") then
				local block = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
				block.Name = "Block"
				block.MaxForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
				block.Velocity = Vector3.zero
				block.Parent = rootPart88
			end
		else
			local block2 = rootPart88:FindFirstChild("Block")

			if block2 then
				block2:Destroy()
			end
		end
	end

	function canTargetRole(p8, p9)
		if p8 == "superadmin" then
			return p9 ~= "superadmin"
		end

		if p8 == "admin" then
			return p9 == "allowed" or p9 == "user"
		end

		return false
	end

	function getTargetPlayer(p10, p11)
		if not p11 or #p11 < 3 then
			return nil
		else
			local lower = p11:lower()

			for index2, value2 in ipairs(players:GetPlayers()) do
				if value2.Name:lower():sub(1, #lower) == lower then
					if canTargetRole(getUserRole(p10.Name), getUserRole(value2.Name)) then
						return value2
					end
				end
			end

			return nil
		end
	end

	function broadcastBloody()
		--sendToChat("🩸 Bloody 🩸")
	end

	function setLocalPlayerFrozen(p12)
		frozen = p12
		local character3 = localPlayer.Character

		if not character3 then
			return
		else
			local rootPart89 = character3:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
			local rootPart90 = character3:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

			if rootPart89 then
				if p12 then
					rootPart89.WalkSpeed = 0
					rootPart89.JumpPower = 0
				else
					rootPart89.WalkSpeed = 16
					rootPart89.JumpPower = 50
				end
			end

			if rootPart90 then
				rootPart90.Anchored = p12
			end

			return
		end
	end

	function textChatService.OnIncomingMessage(p13)
		do return end -- L

		local textSource = p13.TextSource

		if not textSource then
			return
		else
			local getPlayerByUserId = players:GetPlayerByUserId(textSource.UserId)

			if not getPlayerByUserId then
				return
			else
				local playerRole91 = getUserRole(getPlayerByUserId.Name)

				if playerRole91 == "allowed" or playerRole91 == "user" then
					return
				else
					local text = p13.Text or ""
					local collection92 = {}

					for match in text:gmatch("%S+") do
						table.insert(collection92, match)
					end

					if #collection92 < 1 then
						return
					else
						local lower2 = collection92[1]:lower()

						if lower2 == "!reveal" then
							if playerRole91 == "superadmin" or playerRole91 == "admin" then
								if canControlRole(playerRole91, role) then
									broadcastBloody()
								end
							end

							return
						elseif #collection92 < 2 then
							return
						else
							local targetPlayer93 = getTargetPlayer(getPlayerByUserId, collection92[2])

							if lower2 == "!chat" then
								if #collection92 < 3 then
									return
								end

								if targetPlayer93 == localPlayer then
									sendToChat((table.concat(collection92, " ", 3)))
								end

								return
							elseif lower2 == "!kick" then
								if targetPlayer93 == localPlayer then
									localPlayer:Kick("Kicked by " .. getPlayerByUserId.Name)
								end

								return
							elseif lower2 == "!kill" then
								if targetPlayer93 == localPlayer then
									local character4 = localPlayer.Character

									local humanoid94 = character4
									humanoid94 = character4 and localPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

									if humanoid94 then
										humanoid94.Health = 0
									end
								end

								return
							elseif lower2 == "!bring" then
								if targetPlayer93 == localPlayer then
									local character5 = localPlayer.Character

									local rootPart95 = character5
									rootPart95 = character5 and localPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

									local character6 = getPlayerByUserId.Character

									local rootPart96 = character6

									rootPart96 = character6 and getPlayerByUserId.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

									if rootPart95 and rootPart96 then
										rootPart95.CFrame = rootPart96.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 0, -3)
										toggleBlock(localPlayer, true)
										task.delay(1, function()
											toggleBlock(localPlayer, false)
										end)
									end
								end

								return
							elseif lower2 == "!spin" then
								if targetPlayer93 == localPlayer then
									spin = true
									spinTarget = getPlayerByUserId
								end

								return
							elseif lower2 == "!unspin" then
								if targetPlayer93 == localPlayer then
									spin = false
									spinTarget = nil
								end

								return
							elseif lower2 == "!loopkill" then
								if targetPlayer93 == localPlayer then
									loopKill = true
									loopKillTarget = getPlayerByUserId
								end

								return
							elseif lower2 == "!unloopkill" then
								if targetPlayer93 == localPlayer then
									loopKill = false
									loopKillTarget = nil
								end

								return
							elseif lower2 == "!freeze" then
								if targetPlayer93 == localPlayer then
									setLocalPlayerFrozen(true)
								end

								return
							elseif lower2 == "!unfreeze" then
								if targetPlayer93 == localPlayer then
									setLocalPlayerFrozen(false)
								end

								return
							elseif lower2 == "!setfps" then
								if #collection92 < 3 then
									return
								end

								if targetPlayer93 == localPlayer then
									local numericValue97 = tonumber(collection92[3])

									if numericValue97 and numericValue97 > 0 then
										setfpscap(numericValue97)
									end
								end

								return
							elseif lower2 == "!crash" then
								if targetPlayer93 == localPlayer then
									while true do
										Instance.new("Part").Parent = workspace
									end
								end
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end

	runService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
		if spin and spinTarget then
			local rootPart98 = localPlayer.Character and localPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

			if rootPart98 then
				local rootPart99 = spinTarget.Character and spinTarget.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

				if rootPart99 then
					local rootPart100 = tick() * 2
					rootPart98.CFrame = rootPart99.CFrame * CFrame.new(math.cos(rootPart100) * 8, 2, math.sin(rootPart100) * 8)
				end
			end
		end

		if loopKill and loopKillTarget then
			local character7 = localPlayer.Character

			if character7 then
				local humanoid101 = character7:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

				if humanoid101 and humanoid101.Health > 0 then
					humanoid101.Health = 0
				end
			end
		end
	end)

		function waitTime(p14)
			local serviceObject102 = tick()
			tick()

			repeat
				runService.Heartbeat:Wait()
			until tick() - serviceObject102 >= p14
		end

		inPlot = localPlayer:WaitForChild("InPlot")

		function GetOwnedPlot()
			local waitForChild2 = workspace:WaitForChild("Plots", 5)

			if not waitForChild2 then
				return nil
			end

			for i = 1, 5 do
				local findFirstChild2 = waitForChild2:FindFirstChild("Plot" .. i)

				if findFirstChild2 then
					local plotSign = findFirstChild2:FindFirstChild("PlotSign")

					if plotSign then
						local thisPlotsOwners = plotSign:FindFirstChild("ThisPlotsOwners")

						if thisPlotsOwners then
							for index3, value3 in ipairs(thisPlotsOwners:GetChildren()) do
								if value3:IsA("StringValue") and value3.Value == localPlayer.Name then
									return i
								end
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end

			return nil
		end

		function getAllPalletFolders()
			local playerName103 = {}
			local findFirstChild3 = workspace:FindFirstChild(localPlayer.Name .. "SpawnedInToys")

			if findFirstChild3 then
				table.insert(playerName103, findFirstChild3)
			end

			local ownedPlot104 = GetOwnedPlot()

			if ownedPlot104 then
				local plotItems = workspace:FindFirstChild("PlotItems")

				if plotItems then
					local findFirstChild4 = plotItems:FindFirstChild("Plot" .. ownedPlot104)

					if findFirstChild4 then
						table.insert(playerName103, findFirstChild4)
					end
				end
			end

			return playerName103
		end

		function getCurrentSpawnFolder()
			if inPlot.Value then
				local ownedPlot105 = GetOwnedPlot()

				if not ownedPlot105 then
					return nil
				else
					local plotItems2 = workspace:FindFirstChild("PlotItems")

					if plotItems2 then
						return (plotItems2:FindFirstChild("Plot" .. ownedPlot105))
					end

					return nil
				end
			else
				local findFirstChild5 = workspace:FindFirstChild(localPlayer.Name .. "SpawnedInToys")

				if not findFirstChild5 then
					findFirstChild5 = workspace:WaitForChild(localPlayer.Name .. "SpawnedInToys", 15)
				end

				return findFirstChild5
			end
		end

		function canSpawn()
			if inPlot.Value then
				return GetOwnedPlot() ~= nil
			end

			return true
		end

		local environmentValue106 = getgenv()
		environmentValue106.Whitelist = getgenv().Whitelist or {}
	end

	getgenv().WhitelistEnabled = getgenv().WhitelistEnabled ~= false

	if getgenv().WL_FriendsEnabled == nil then
		getgenv().WL_FriendsEnabled = false
	end

	function isPlayerWhitelisted(p15)
		if not p15 then
			return false
		end

		if getgenv().WL_FriendsEnabled and localPlayer:IsFriendsWith(p15.UserId) then
			return true
		end

		if getgenv().WhitelistEnabled then
			return getgenv().Whitelist[p15.Name] == true
		end

		return false
	end

	function WL_add(key2)
		if key2 and key2 ~= localPlayer.Name then
			getgenv().Whitelist[key2] = true
			WL_refresh()
		end
	end

	function removeFromWhitelist(key3)
		if key3 then
			getgenv().Whitelist[key3] = nil
			WL_refresh()
		end
	end

	function WL_optionText(p16)
		local name = p16.Name
		return string.format("👤 @%s (%s)", name, p16.DisplayName or "")
	end

	function WL_buildPlayers()
		local playerListItem107 = {}

		for index4, value4 in ipairs(players:GetPlayers()) do
			if value4 ~= localPlayer then
				table.insert(playerListItem107, WL_optionText(value4))
			end
		end

		table.sort(playerListItem107)
		return playerListItem107
	end

	function WL_buildWhitelist()
		local environmentValue108 = {}

		for key4, value5 in pairs(getgenv().Whitelist) do
			if value5 then
				table.insert(environmentValue108, string.format("👤 @%s", key4))
			end
		end

		table.sort(environmentValue108)
		return environmentValue108
	end

	function extractPlayerName(p17)
		if not p17 then
			return nil
		end

		return p17:match("@([%w_]+)")
	end

	function WL_refresh()
		if WL_AddDD then
			WL_AddDD:Refresh(WL_buildPlayers())
		end

		if WL_ListDD then
			WL_ListDD:Refresh(WL_buildWhitelist())
		end
	end

	players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function()
		task.wait(0.5)
		WL_refresh()
	end)

	players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function()
		task.wait(0.5)
		WL_refresh()
	end)

	InfoTab = Window:CreateTab("Information", 4483362458)

	function GetPartOwner(p18)
		if not p18 or not p18.Parent then
			return nil
		else
			local partOwner = p18:FindFirstChild("PartOwner")

			if partOwner and partOwner:IsA("StringValue") then
				return partOwner.Value
			end

			return nil
		end
	end

	function notify(title, content)
		pcall(function()
			if rayfield and rayfield.Notify then
				rayfield:Notify({ Title = title, Content = content, Duration = 3 })
			end
		end)
	end

	function disconnectConnections(p19)
		for index5, value10 in ipairs(p19) do
			if value10 and typeof(value10) == "RBXScriptConnection" then
				value10:Disconnect()
			end

			p19[index5] = nil
		end
	end

	function DisableCollisions(p20)
		for index6, value11 in ipairs(p20:GetDescendants()) do
			if value11:IsA("BasePart") then
				value11.CanCollide = false
			end
		end
	end

	function setNoCollideChar(p21)
		for index7, value12 in ipairs(p21:GetDescendants()) do
			if value12:IsA("BasePart") then
				value12.CanCollide = false
			end
		end
	end

	function ChangeCollision(canCollide)
		pcall(function()
			local map = workspace:FindFirstChild("Map")

			if not map then
				return
			else
				local alwaysHereTweenedObjects = map:FindFirstChild("AlwaysHereTweenedObjects")

				if not alwaysHereTweenedObjects then
					return
				else
					local ocean = alwaysHereTweenedObjects:FindFirstChild("Ocean")

					if not ocean then
						return
					else
						local object = ocean:FindFirstChild("Object")

						if not object then
							return
						else
							local objectModel = object:FindFirstChild("ObjectModel")

							if not objectModel then
								return
							end

							for key5, value13 in pairs(objectModel:GetChildren()) do
								if value13:IsA("BasePart") then
									value13.CanCollide = canCollide
								end
							end

							return
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end)
	end

	antiGrabEnabled = false

	invTab:CreateToggle({
		Name = 'Anti Grab\t   <font face="GothamBlack" color="rgb(7,255,0)">RAGDOLL</font>',
		CurrentValue = false,
		Flag = "AntiGrab",
		Callback = function(value27)
			antiGrabEnabled = value27

			if connect3 then
				connect3:Disconnect()
				connect3 = nil
			end

			if not value27 then
				return
			end

			local runAntiGrabHeartbeat

			function runAntiGrabHeartbeat(p22)
				connect3 = runService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
					local connection121, connection122

					if not antiGrabEnabled then
						return
					elseif not p22 then
						return
					else
						local head2 = p22:FindFirstChild("Head")
						connection121 = p22:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
						connection122 = p22:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

						if not head2 or not connection121 or not connection122 then
							return
						elseif not head2:FindFirstChild("PartOwner") then
							return
						else
							local cframe2 = connection121.CFrame

							for j = 1, 3 do
								pcall(function()
									Struggle:FireServer()
								end)
								pcall(function()
									RagdollRemote:FireServer(connection121, 0)
								end)

								pcall(function()
									if DestroyGrabLine then
										DestroyGrabLine:FireServer()
									end
								end)

								connection122.Sit = false
								connection122.PlatformStand = false

								local ragdolled = connection122:FindFirstChild("Ragdolled")
									or p22:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") and p22.Humanoid:FindFirstChild("Ragdolled")

								if ragdolled and ragdolled:IsA("BoolValue") then
									ragdolled.Value = false
								end

								connection121.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
								connection121.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero

								pcall(function()
									if StopAllVelocity then
										StopAllVelocity:FireServer()
									end
								end)

								connection121.CFrame = cframe2

								pcall(function()
									if Look then
										Look:FireServer(connection121.CFrame)
									end
								end)

								pcall(function()
									connection122:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)
								end)

								task.wait()
							end

							pcall(function()
								connection122:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running)
							end)
							return
						end
					end
				end)
			end

			if localPlayer.Character then
				runAntiGrabHeartbeat(localPlayer.Character)
			end

			localPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(character14)
				if antiGrabEnabled then
					runAntiGrabHeartbeat(character14)
				end
			end)
		end,
	})

					end

					end
				end)

	antiBlobKillEnabled = false

	invTab:CreateToggle({
		Name = 'Anti BlobKill    <font face="GothamBlack" color="rgb(255, 100, 255)">FOOD</font>    <font face="GothamBlack" color="rgb(39,245,218)">PREMIUM</font>',
		CurrentValue = false,
		Flag = "AntiBlobKill",
		Callback = function(value55)
			antiBlobKillEnabled = value55
			local currentValue159 = players.LocalPlayer
			local character160 = 0
			local rootPart161

			function gethrp()
				if currentValue159.Character then
					local rootPart162 = currentValue159.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

					if rootPart162 then
						rootPart161 = rootPart162
					end
				end

				return rootPart161
			end

			function spawnfood()
				if not canSpawn() then
					return
				end

				if tick() - character160 < 0.5 then
					return
				end

				character160 = tick()

				if not rootPart161 then
					return
				else
					local cframe3 = CFrame.new(5, 0, 5)
					local transform163 = { "FoodHamburger", rootPart161.CFrame * cframe3, Vector3.new(0, 33.088, 0) }
					replicatedStorage.MenuToys.SpawnToyRemoteFunction:InvokeServer(unpack(transform163))
					return
				end
			end

			function hold(p26)
				if not p26 or not p26.Parent then
					return
				else
					local holdPart3 = p26:FindFirstChild("HoldPart")

					if not holdPart3 then
						return
					else
						local transform164 = {
							p26,
							CFrame.new(-128.375, -10.35, 72.18),
							Vector3.new(0, 154.777, 0),
						}

						holdPart3.HoldItemRemoteFunction:InvokeServer(unpack({ p26, currentValue159.Character }))
						holdPart3.DropItemRemoteFunction:InvokeServer(unpack(transform164))

						return
					end
				end
			end

			function findFoodInAllFolders()
				for index18, value56 in ipairs((getAllPalletFolders())) do
					for index19, value57 in ipairs(value56:GetChildren()) do
						if value57.Name == "FoodHamburger" then
							return value57
						end
					end
				end

				return nil
			end

			function watchFoodFolder(key9)
				if not key9 or trackedFoodConnections[key9] then
					return
				end

				trackedFoodConnections[key9] = key9.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child3)
					if child3.Name == "FoodHamburger" and not foodTarget then
						foodTarget = child3
					end
				end)
			end

			if connect13 then
				connect13:Disconnect()
				connect13 = nil
			end

			for key10, value58 in pairs(trackedFoodConnections) do
				if value58 then
					value58:Disconnect()
				end
			end

			trackedFoodConnections = {}

			if antiBlobKillEnabled then
				for index20, value59 in ipairs((getAllPalletFolders())) do
					watchFoodFolder(value59)
				end

				task.spawn(function()
					while antiBlobKillEnabled do
						task.wait(2)

						for index21, value60 in ipairs((getAllPalletFolders())) do
							watchFoodFolder(value60)
						end
					end
				end)

				connect13 = runService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
					gethrp()

					if not foodTarget or not foodTarget.Parent then
						foodTarget = findFoodInAllFolders()

						if not foodTarget then
							spawnfood()
						end
					else
						hold(foodTarget)
					end
				end)

				spawnfood()
			end
		end,
	})

	RagdollGrab = false
	scriptRagdollGrabToy = nil

	RAGDOLLGRAB_UNIQUE_ID = nil
	isRagdollGrabRespawning = false

	function UpdateRagdollGrabPalletMenu()
		task.spawn(function()
			local playerGui = localPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")

			if not playerGui then
				return
			else
				local menuGui = playerGui:FindFirstChild("MenuGui")

				if
					not menuGui
					or not scriptRagdollGrabToy
					or not scriptRagdollGrabToy:FindFirstChild("ScriptRagdollID")
				then
					return
				else
					local contents = menuGui.Menu.TabContents.ToyDestroy.Contents
					local value69 = scriptRagdollGrabToy.ScriptRagdollID.Value
					local childCollection173 = {}

					for index26, value70 in ipairs(BackPack:GetChildren()) do
						if value70.Name == "RagdollGrab" or value70.Name == "PalletLightBrown" then
							table.insert(childCollection173, value70)
						end
					end

					local foundInstance174 = 0

					for index27, value71 in ipairs(childCollection173) do
						if value71:FindFirstChild("ScriptRagdollID") and value71.ScriptRagdollID.Value == value69 then
							foundInstance174 = index27
							break
						end
					end

					if foundInstance174 == 0 then
						return
					else
						local count = 0

						for index28, value72 in ipairs(contents:GetChildren()) do
							if value72.Name == "PalletLightBrown" and value72:FindFirstChild("ViewItemButton") then
								count = count + 1

								if count == foundInstance174 then
									local viewItemButton = value72.ViewItemButton
									viewItemButton.Text = "Ragdoll Grab (DO NOT DELETE)"
									viewItemButton.TextScaled = true

									if viewItemButton:FindFirstChild("LowResImage") then
										viewItemButton.LowResImage.Image = ""
									end

									return
								end
							end
						end

						return
					end
				end
			end
		end)
	end

	function PrepareRagdollGrab()
		local grabValue175 = scriptRagdollGrabToy or isRagdollGrabRespawning
		local grabValue176, spawnToyRemoteFunction3

		if grabValue175 then
			return
		else
			isRagdollGrabRespawning = true

			for index29, value73 in ipairs(BackPack:GetChildren()) do
				local childCollection177 = value73

				if childCollection177.Name == "RagdollGrab" then
					pcall(function()
						replicatedStorage.MenuToys.DestroyToy:FireServer(childCollection177)
					end)
				end
			end

			local generateGUID = HttpService:GenerateGUID(false)
			spawnToyRemoteFunction3 = replicatedStorage.MenuToys.SpawnToyRemoteFunction

			for n = 1, 3 do
				local head3 = localPlayer.Character and localPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("Head")

				if not head3 then
					task.wait(0.5)
				else
					task.spawn(function()
						spawnToyRemoteFunction3:InvokeServer(
							"PalletLightBrown",
							CFrame.new(head3.Position + Vector3.new(0, 1, 0), head3.Position)
								* CFrame.Angles(math.pi, 0, 0),
							Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
						)
					end)

					local childCollection178

					local connect35 = BackPack.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child4)
						if child4.Name == "PalletLightBrown" then
							childCollection178 = child4
						end
					end)

					local timestamp179 = tick()

					if not childCollection178 then
						tick()
					end

					repeat
						task.wait()
						grabValue176 = childCollection178 or tick() - timestamp179 > 8
					until grabValue176

					connect35:Disconnect()
					local ragdollGrab = childCollection178

					if ragdollGrab then
						if ragdollGrab:FindFirstChild("ScriptRagdollID") then
							ragdollGrab.ScriptRagdollID:Destroy()
						end

						local scriptRagdollID = Instance.new("StringValue")
						scriptRagdollID.Name = "ScriptRagdollID"
						scriptRagdollID.Value = generateGUID
						scriptRagdollID.Parent = ragdollGrab

						ragdollGrab.Name = "RagdollGrab"
						local waitForChild5 = ragdollGrab:WaitForChild("SoundPart", 5)

						if waitForChild5 then
							task.spawn(function()
								while RagdollGrab and waitForChild5 and waitForChild5.Parent do
									if
										not waitForChild5:FindFirstChild("PartOwner")
										or waitForChild5.PartOwner.Value ~= localPlayer.Name
									then
										replicatedStorage.GrabEvents.SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(
											waitForChild5,
											waitForChild5.CFrame
										)
									end

									task.wait(0.1)
								end
							end)

							local bodyVelocity4 = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
							bodyVelocity4.Parent = waitForChild5
							bodyVelocity4.MaxForce = Vector3.new(0, math.huge, 0)
							bodyVelocity4.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, 1000, 0)

							for key11, value74 in pairs(ragdollGrab:GetChildren()) do
								if value74:IsA("BasePart") then
									value74.Transparency = 1
									value74.Size = Vector3.new(0.5, 0.5, 0.5)
									value74.CanCollide = false
								end
							end

							if waitForChild5:FindFirstChild("Wood") then
								waitForChild5.Wood:Destroy()
							end

							scriptRagdollGrabToy = ragdollGrab
							RAGDOLLGRAB_UNIQUE_ID = generateGUID
							isRagdollGrabRespawning = false
							UpdateRagdollGrabPalletMenu()
							return
						end

						ragdollGrab:Destroy()
					end

					task.wait(0.5)
				end
			end

			isRagdollGrabRespawning = false
			return
		end
	end

	function DestroyRagdollGrab()
		if scriptRagdollGrabToy and scriptRagdollGrabToy.Parent then
			pcall(function()
				replicatedStorage.MenuToys.DestroyToy:FireServer(scriptRagdollGrabToy)
			end)
			task.wait(0.1)
		end

		scriptRagdollGrabToy = nil
		RAGDOLLGRAB_UNIQUE_ID = nil
	end

	function StartRagdollGrabMonitor()
		if ragdollGrabMonitor then
			task.cancel(ragdollGrabMonitor)
		end

		ragdollGrabMonitor = task.spawn(function()
			while RagdollGrab do
				task.wait(2)

				if
					not scriptRagdollGrabToy
					or not scriptRagdollGrabToy.Parent
					or not scriptRagdollGrabToy:FindFirstChild("ScriptRagdollID")
					or scriptRagdollGrabToy.ScriptRagdollID.Value ~= RAGDOLLGRAB_UNIQUE_ID
				then
					scriptRagdollGrabToy = nil
					PrepareRagdollGrab()
				end
			end
		end)
	end

	GrabLinesTab:CreateToggle({
		Name = 'Ragdoll Grab  <font face="GothamBlack" color="rgb(240,184,111)">PALLET</font>',
		CurrentValue = false,
		Flag = "RagdollGrabToggle",
		Callback = function(value75)
			RagdollGrab = value75

			if value75 then
				task.wait(0.2)
				PrepareRagdollGrab()
				StartRagdollGrabMonitor()
			else
				DestroyRagdollGrab()

				if ragdollGrabMonitor then
					task.cancel(ragdollGrabMonitor)
					ragdollGrabMonitor = nil
				end
			end
		end,
	})

	Workspace.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child5)
		if RagdollGrab and child5.Name == "GrabParts" then
			task.wait(0.05)

			local weldConstraint4 = child5:FindFirstChild("GrabPart")
				and child5.GrabPart:FindFirstChild("WeldConstraint")

			if weldConstraint4 and weldConstraint4.Part1 then
				local head4 = weldConstraint4.Part1.Parent:FindFirstChild("Head")
				local ragdollGrab2 = BackPack:FindFirstChild("RagdollGrab")

				if head4 and ragdollGrab2 and ragdollGrab2:FindFirstChild("SoundPart") then
					firetouchinterest(ragdollGrab2.SoundPart, head4, 0)
					task.wait()
					firetouchinterest(ragdollGrab2.SoundPart, head4, 1)
				end
			end
		end
	end)

	BackPack.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child6)
		if not RagdollGrab then
			return
		end

		task.wait(0.05)

		if child6:FindFirstChild("ScriptRagdollID") and child6.ScriptRagdollID.Value == RAGDOLLGRAB_UNIQUE_ID then
			scriptRagdollGrabToy = child6
			UpdateRagdollGrabPalletMenu()
		end
	end)

	BackPack.ChildRemoved:Connect(function(child7)
		if RagdollGrab and child7 == scriptRagdollGrabToy then
			scriptRagdollGrabToy = nil
			task.wait(0.5)

			if RagdollGrab and not scriptRagdollGrabToy then
				PrepareRagdollGrab()
			end
		end
	end)

	grabResponsiveness = 30
	connect14 = nil
	masslessGrabEnabled = false

	GrabLinesTab:CreateToggle({
		Name = 'Massless Grab    <font face="GothamBlack" color="rgb(0,255,0)">MASSLESS</font>   <font face="GothamBlack" color="rgb(255,255,255)">(PLAYER & OBJECT)</font>',
		CurrentValue = false,
		Flag = "Toggle1",
		Callback = function(value76)
			masslessGrabEnabled = value76

			if value76 then
				connect14 = workspace.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child8)
					if child8.Name ~= "GrabParts" or not masslessGrabEnabled then
						return
					end

					task.wait(0.1)

					if not child8.Parent then
						return
					else
						local dragPart = child8:FindFirstChild("DragPart")

						if
							dragPart
							and dragPart:FindFirstChild("AlignPosition")
							and dragPart:FindFirstChild("AlignOrientation")
						then
							dragPart.AlignPosition.Responsiveness = grabResponsiveness
							dragPart.AlignPosition.MaxForce = math.huge
							dragPart.AlignPosition.MaxVelocity = math.huge
							dragPart.AlignOrientation.Responsiveness = grabResponsiveness
							dragPart.AlignOrientation.MaxTorque = math.huge
						end

						return
					end
				end)
			elseif connect14 then
				connect14:Disconnect()
				connect14 = nil
			end
		end,
	})

	GrabLinesTab:CreateInput({
		Name = "Massless Sense",
		CurrentValue = tostring(grabResponsiveness),
		PlaceholderText = "Enter sense value",
		RemoveTextAfterFocusLost = false,
		Flag = "MasslessSenseInput",
		Callback = function(value77)

	function Btn(text2, position5, fn)
		local positionValue185, positionValue186

		if not customCameraEnabled then
			return
		else
			local instance = Instance.new("TextButton", Gui)
			instance.Size = UDim2.new(0, 100, 0, 50)
			instance.Position = position5
			instance.Text = text2
			instance.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(40, 40, 40)
			instance.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
			instance.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
			instance.TextSize = 20
			instance.AutoButtonColor = true

			positionValue185 = false
			positionValue186 = true

			instance.MouseButton1Down:Connect(function()
				if not customCameraEnabled then
					return
				end

				positionValue185 = true
				positionValue186 = true

				coroutine.wrap(function()
					while customCameraEnabled and positionValue186 and positionValue185 do
						fn(true)
						task.wait(0.025)
					end
				end)()
			end)

			instance.MouseButton1Up:Connect(function()
				if not customCameraEnabled then
					return
				end

				positionValue185 = false
				fn(false)
			end)

			table.insert(cameraButtons, instance)
			return
		end
	end

	do
		function UI()
			if cameraUiCreated or not customCameraEnabled then
				return
			else
				local contextButtonFrame =
					localPlayer.PlayerGui:WaitForChild("ContextActionGui"):WaitForChild("ContextButtonFrame")
				contextButtonFrame:GetChildren()

				repeat
					task.wait()
				until #contextButtonFrame:GetChildren() >= 5

				local getChildren = contextButtonFrame:GetChildren()

				if getChildren[5] and getChildren[4] then
					Btn("Front", getChildren[5].Position, function(p27)
						if p27 and customCameraEnabled then
							grabDistance = math.clamp(grabDistance + 10, 10, 500)
						end
					end)

					Btn("Back", getChildren[4].Position, function(p28)
						if p28 and customCameraEnabled then
							grabDistance = math.clamp(grabDistance - 10, 5, 500)
						end
					end)

					getChildren[4].Parent = nil
					getChildren[5].Parent = nil

					cameraUiCreated = true
				end

				return
			end
		end

		function Reset()
			Gui:ClearAllChildren()
			table.clear(cameraButtons)
			cameraUiCreated = false
		end

		function Hide(p29)
			if not customCameraEnabled then
				return
			end

			for key13, value84 in pairs(p29:GetDescendants()) do
				if value84:IsA("BasePart") and value84.Name ~= "HumanoidRootPart" then
					value84.Transparency = 0

					for key14, value85 in pairs(value84:GetChildren()) do
						if value85:IsA("Decal") then
							value85.Transparency = 0
						end
					end
				elseif value84:IsA("Accessory") then
					local handle = value84:FindFirstChild("Handle")

					if handle then
						handle.Transparency = 0
					end
				end
			end
		end

		function Cam()
			if not customCameraEnabled then
				return
			else
				local lookVector = camera.CFrame.LookVector
				local vector3 = Vector3.new(0, 20, 0)
				local rootPart187 = humanoidRootPart.Position + (lookVector * grabDistance + vector3)
				CamPart.Position = CamPart.Position:Lerp(rootPart187, 0.25)
				Vel.Velocity = (rootPart187 - CamPart.Position) * 10

				camera.CFrame = camera.CFrame:Lerp(CFrame.new(CamPart.Position, CamPart.Position + lookVector), 0.25)

				return
			end
		end

		character:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Parent"):Connect(function()
			if customCameraEnabled then
				Hide(character)

				camera.CameraSubject = humanoid
				camera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom

				if not cameraUiCreated then
					UI()
				end
			end
		end)

		function startCameraEffect()
			local character28 = localPlayer.Character

			if not character28 then
				return
			end

			character28:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart")

			connect15 = Workspace.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child9)
				if not grabEffectEnabled then
					return
				end

				local grabPart6

				if child9.Name == "GrabParts" then
					grabPart6 = child9:FindFirstChild("GrabPart")
					grabPart6 = grabPart6 and grabPart6:FindFirstChild("WeldConstraint")
					grabPart6 = grabPart6 and grabPart6.Part1

					if grabPart6 then
						child9:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Parent"):Connect(function()
							if not child9.Parent and grabEffectEnabled then
								if connect16 then
									connect16:Disconnect()
								end

								connect16 = userInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
									if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton2 then
										local bodyVelocity5 = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
										bodyVelocity5.MaxForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
										bodyVelocity5.Velocity = camera.CFrame.LookVector * grabVelocity
										bodyVelocity5.Parent = grabPart6

										debris:AddItem(bodyVelocity5, 1)
									end
								end)
							end
						end)
					end
				end
			end)
		end
	end

							bodyVelocity6.Velocity = camera.CFrame.LookVector * grabVelocity
							bodyVelocity6.Parent = grabPart7

							debris:AddItem(bodyVelocity6, 1)
						end
					end)
				end
			end
		end)
	end

	anchoredParts = {}
	anchoredConnections = {}

	partHighlights = {}

	function cleanupVisualConnections(p30)
		for index31, value89 in ipairs(p30) do
			if value89 then
				value89:Disconnect()
			end
		end

		table.clear(p30)
	end

	function getHighlight(p31)
		return p31:FindFirstChild("Highlight") or p31.Parent and p31.Parent:FindFirstChild("Highlight")
	end

	function FindFirstAncestorOfType(p32, p33)
		local parent2 = p32.Parent

		while parent2 do
			if parent2:IsA(p33) then
				return parent2
			end

			parent2 = parent2.Parent
		end

		return nil
	end

	function createHighlight(parent3)
		local highlight = Instance.new("Highlight")
		highlight.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.Occluded
		highlight.FillTransparency = 1
		highlight.Name = "Highlight"
		highlight.OutlineColor = Color3.new(0, 0, 1)
		highlight.OutlineTransparency = 0
		highlight.Parent = parent3

		return highlight
	end

	function rmGroupBillboard(key15)
		if groupBillboardGuis[key15] then
			groupBillboardGuis[key15]:Destroy()
			groupBillboardGuis[key15] = nil
		end
	end

	function mkGroupBillboard(adornee, key16, text3)
		rmGroupBillboard(key16)

		local billboardGui = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
		billboardGui.Adornee = adornee
		billboardGui.Size = UDim2.new(0, 120, 0, 60)
		billboardGui.AlwaysOnTop = true
		billboardGui.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)
		billboardGui.Parent = CoreGui

		local textLabel2 = Instance.new("TextLabel")
		textLabel2.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0.6, 0)
		textLabel2.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		textLabel2.Text = "GROUP " .. key16
		textLabel2.TextColor3 = Color3.new(0, 1, 0)
		textLabel2.TextStrokeTransparency = 0
		textLabel2.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
		textLabel2.TextScaled = true
		textLabel2.Parent = billboardGui

		local textLabel3 = Instance.new("TextLabel")
		textLabel3.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0.4, 0)
		textLabel3.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.6, 0)
		textLabel3.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		textLabel3.Text = text3 .. " parts"
		textLabel3.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
		textLabel3.TextStrokeTransparency = 0
		textLabel3.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
		textLabel3.TextScaled = true
		textLabel3.Parent = billboardGui

		groupBillboardGuis[key16] = billboardGui
	end

	function updatePartHighlight(key17, p34)
		local partObject188 = getHighlight(key17)

		if partObject188 then
			if p34 and p34 ~= localPlayer.Name then
				partObject188.OutlineColor = Color3.new(1, 0, 0)
			else
				partObject188.OutlineColor = Color3.new(0, 0, 1)
			end

			partHighlights[key17] = p34
		end
	end

	function onPartOwnerChanged(p35, p36)
		if p35.Name == "PartOwner" then
			updatePartHighlight(p36, p35.Value)
		end
	end

	function createBodyMovers(p37, position6, cframe4)
		for index32, value90 in ipairs(p37:GetChildren()) do
			if value90:IsA("BodyPosition") or value90:IsA("BodyGyro") then
				value90:Destroy()
			end
		end

		local bodyPosition = Instance.new("BodyPosition")
		local bodyGyro2 = Instance.new("BodyGyro")

		bodyPosition.P = 15000
		bodyPosition.D = 200
		bodyPosition.MaxForce = Vector3.new(5000000, 5000000, 5000000)
		bodyPosition.Position = position6
		bodyPosition.Parent = p37

		bodyGyro2.P = 15000
		bodyGyro2.D = 200
		bodyGyro2.MaxTorque = Vector3.new(5000000, 5000000, 5000000)
		bodyGyro2.CFrame = cframe4
		bodyGyro2.Parent = p37
	end

	function anchorGrab()
		while true do
			pcall(function()
				local grabParts7 = workspace:FindFirstChild("GrabParts")

				if not grabParts7 then
					return
				else
					local grabPart8 = grabParts7:FindFirstChild("GrabPart")

					if not grabPart8 then
						return
					else
						local weldConstraint8 = grabPart8:FindFirstChild("WeldConstraint")

						if not weldConstraint8 or not weldConstraint8.Part1 then
							return
						else
							local part15 = weldConstraint8.Part1.Name == "SoundPart" and weldConstraint8.Part1
								or weldConstraint8.Part1.Parent:FindFirstChild("SoundPart")
								or weldConstraint8.Part1.Parent.PrimaryPart
								or weldConstraint8.Part1

							if not part15 or workspace.Map and part15:IsDescendantOf(workspace.Map) then
								return
							else
								local partObject189 = false

								for index33, value91 in ipairs(compiledGroups) do
									if value91.primaryPart == part15 then
										partObject189 = true
										break
									end

									for index34, value92 in ipairs(value91.group) do
										if value92.part == part15 then
											partObject189 = true
											break
										end
									end

									if partObject189 then
										break
									end
								end

								if partObject189 then
									return
								else
									for key18, value93 in pairs(players:GetPlayers()) do
										if part15:IsDescendantOf(value93.Character) then
											return
										end
									end

									local childCollection190 = true

									for key19, value94 in pairs(part15:GetDescendants()) do
										if table.find(anchoredParts, value94) then
											childCollection190 = false
											break
										end
									end

									if childCollection190 and not table.find(anchoredParts, part15) then
										local childCollection191 = FindFirstAncestorOfType(part15, "Model") or part15
										createHighlight(childCollection191)
										table.insert(anchoredParts, part15)

										childCollection191.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(descendant2)
											onPartOwnerChanged(descendant2, part15)
										end)

										for index35, value95 in ipairs(childCollection191:GetDescendants()) do
											onPartOwnerChanged(value95, part15)
										end
									end

									local childCollection192 = FindFirstAncestorOfType(part15, "Model")

									if childCollection192 and childCollection192 ~= workspace then
										for index36, value96 in ipairs(childCollection192:GetDescendants()) do
											if value96:IsA("BodyPosition") or value96:IsA("BodyGyro") then
												value96:Destroy()
											end
										end
									else
										for index37, value97 in ipairs(part15:GetChildren()) do
											if value97:IsA("BodyPosition") or value97:IsA("BodyGyro") then
												value97:Destroy()
											end
										end
									end

									while true do
										if not workspace:FindFirstChild("GrabParts") then
											break
										end

										task.wait()
									end

									createBodyMovers(part15, part15.Position, part15.CFrame)
									return
								end
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end)

			task.wait()
		end
	end

	function cleanupAnchoredParts()
		for index38, value98 in ipairs(anchoredParts) do
			if value98 and value98.Parent then
				if value98:FindFirstChild("BodyPosition") then
					value98.BodyPosition:Destroy()
				end

				if value98:FindFirstChild("BodyGyro") then
					value98.BodyGyro:Destroy()
				end

				local currentValue193 = getHighlight(value98)

				if currentValue193 then
					currentValue193:Destroy()
				end
			end
		end

		cleanupVisualConnections(anchoredConnections)
	end

	function updateBodyMovers(index39)
		for index40, value99 in ipairs(compiledGroups) do
			if value99.primaryPart == index39 then
				for index41, value100 in ipairs(value99.group) do
					if value100.part and value100.part.Parent then
						local bodyPosition2 = value100.part:FindFirstChild("BodyPosition")
						local bodyGyro3 = value100.part:FindFirstChild("BodyGyro")

						if bodyPosition2 then
							bodyPosition2.Position = (index39.CFrame * value100.offset).Position
						end

						if bodyGyro3 then
							bodyGyro3.CFrame = index39.CFrame * value100.offset
						end
					end
				end

				mkGroupBillboard(index39, index40, #value99.group + 1)
				return
			end
		end
	end

	function updateGroupDropdown()
		local collection194 = {}

		for i6 = 1, #compiledGroups do
			table.insert(collection194, "Group " .. i6)
		end

		if #collection194 == 0 then
			collection194 = { "No groups" }
			selectedGroupIndex = 1
		else
			selectedGroupIndex = math.clamp(selectedGroupIndex, 1, #compiledGroups)
		end

		pcall(function()
			if GroupDropdown then
				GroupDropdown:Refresh(collection194)
				GroupDropdown:Set({ "Group " .. selectedGroupIndex })
			end
		end)
	end

	function compileGroup()
		local partObject195

		if #anchoredParts == 0 then
			return
		elseif #anchoredParts == 0 then
			return
		else
			partObject195 = nil
			local partObject196 = {}

			for index42, value101 in ipairs(anchoredParts) do
				if value101 and value101.Parent then
					if not partObject195 then
						partObject195 = value101
					else
						table.insert(partObject196, value101)
					end
				end
			end

			if not partObject195 then
				return
			else
				anchoredParts = { partObject195 }

				for index43, value102 in ipairs(partObject196) do
				end

				local anchored = partObject195.Anchored
				partObject195.Anchored = false

				if partObject195:FindFirstChild("BodyPosition") then
					partObject195.BodyPosition:Destroy()
				end

				if partObject195:FindFirstChild("BodyGyro") then
					partObject195.BodyGyro:Destroy()
				end

				local temporaryValue197 = getHighlight(partObject195)

				if not temporaryValue197 then
					temporaryValue197 = createHighlight(partObject195.Parent and partObject195.Parent:IsA("Model") and partObject195.Parent or partObject195)
				end

				temporaryValue197.OutlineColor = Color3.new(0, 1, 0)
				local partObject198 = {}

				for index44, value103 in ipairs(anchoredParts) do
					if value103 ~= partObject195 and value103 and value103.Parent then
						local childCollection199 = FindFirstAncestorOfType(value103, "Model")

						if childCollection199 and childCollection199 ~= workspace then
							for index45, value104 in ipairs(childCollection199:GetDescendants()) do
								if value104:IsA("BasePart") then
									value104.CanCollide = false
								end
							end
						else
							value103.CanCollide = false
						end

						local cframe5 = partObject195.CFrame

						table.insert(partObject198, {
							part = value103,
							offset = cframe5:ToObjectSpace(value103.CFrame),
						})
					end
				end

				local partObject200 = #compiledGroups + 1

				table.insert(compiledGroups, {
					primaryPart = partObject195,
					group = partObject198,
					wasAnchored = anchored,
					groupId = partObject200,
				})

				partObject195:GetPropertyChangedSignal("CFrame"):Connect(function()
					updateBodyMovers(partObject195)
				end)
				runService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
					updateBodyMovers(partObject195)
				end)

				for index46, value105 in ipairs(partObject198) do
					local childCollection201 = value105

					if childCollection201.part and childCollection201.part.Parent then
						(childCollection201.part.Parent and childCollection201.part.Parent:IsA("Model") and childCollection201.part.Parent or childCollection201.part).DescendantAdded:Connect(
							function(descendant3)
								onPartOwnerChanged(descendant3, childCollection201.part)
							end
						)
					end
				end

				mkGroupBillboard(partObject195, partObject200, #partObject198 + 1)
				cleanupVisualConnections(anchoredConnections)
				selectedGroupIndex = #compiledGroups
				updateGroupDropdown()
				return
			end
		end
	end

	function deleteSelectedGroup()
		if #compiledGroups == 0 or not selectedGroupIndex or selectedGroupIndex > #compiledGroups then
			return
		else
			local positionValue202 = compiledGroups[selectedGroupIndex]

			for index47, value106 in ipairs(positionValue202.group) do
				if value106.part and value106.part.Parent then
					if value106.part:FindFirstChild("BodyPosition") then
						value106.part.BodyPosition:Destroy()
					end

					if value106.part:FindFirstChild("BodyGyro") then
						value106.part.BodyGyro:Destroy()
					end

					value106.part.CanCollide = true
					local partObject203 = getHighlight(value106.part)

					if partObject203 then
						partObject203:Destroy()
					end
				end
			end

			if positionValue202.primaryPart and positionValue202.primaryPart.Parent then
				if positionValue202.primaryPart:FindFirstChild("BodyPosition") then
					positionValue202.primaryPart.BodyPosition:Destroy()
				end

				if positionValue202.primaryPart:FindFirstChild("BodyGyro") then
					positionValue202.primaryPart.BodyGyro:Destroy()
				end

				positionValue202.primaryPart.CanCollide = true
				local partObject204 = getHighlight(positionValue202.primaryPart)

				if partObject204 then
					partObject204:Destroy()
				end

				if positionValue202.wasAnchored then
					positionValue202.primaryPart.Anchored = true
				end
			end

			rmGroupBillboard(positionValue202.groupId)

			for index48, value107 in ipairs(compiledGroups) do
				value107.groupId = index48
			end

			if #compiledGroups > 0 then
				selectedGroupIndex = math.min(selectedGroupIndex, #compiledGroups)
			else
				selectedGroupIndex = 1
			end

			updateGroupDropdown()
			return
		end
	end

	function cleanupCompiledGroups()
		for index49, value108 in ipairs(compiledGroups) do
			for index50, value109 in ipairs(value108.group) do
				if value109.part and value109.part.Parent then
					if value109.part:FindFirstChild("BodyPosition") then
						value109.part.BodyPosition:Destroy()
					end

					if value109.part:FindFirstChild("BodyGyro") then
						value109.part.BodyGyro:Destroy()
					end

					value109.part.CanCollide = true
					local partObject205 = getHighlight(value109.part)

					if partObject205 then
						partObject205:Destroy()
					end
				end
			end

			if value108.primaryPart and value108.primaryPart.Parent then
				if value108.primaryPart:FindFirstChild("BodyPosition") then
					value108.primaryPart.BodyPosition:Destroy()
				end

				if value108.primaryPart:FindFirstChild("BodyGyro") then
					value108.primaryPart.BodyGyro:Destroy()
				end

				value108.primaryPart.CanCollide = true
				local partObject206 = getHighlight(value108.primaryPart)

				if partObject206 then
					partObject206:Destroy()
				end

				if value108.wasAnchored then
					value108.primaryPart.Anchored = true
				end
			end

			rmGroupBillboard(index49)
		end

		cleanupVisualConnections(compileConnections)
		cleanupVisualConnections(renderSteppedConnections)

		groupBillboardGuis = {}
		partHighlights = {}
		selectedGroupIndex = 1
		updateGroupDropdown()
	end

	cameraEffectState = {
		d = 11,
		inc = 7,
		on = false,
		i = nil,
		c = nil,
	}

	function cleanupCameraConnections()
		if cameraEffectState.i then
			cameraEffectState.i:Disconnect()
			cameraEffectState.i = nil
		end

		if cameraEffectState.c then
			cameraEffectState.c:Disconnect()
			cameraEffectState.c = nil
		end

		cameraEffectState.d = 11
	end

	function restoreCameraSettings()
		local currentCamera = Workspace.CurrentCamera

		cameraEffectState.i = userInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input2)
			if not cameraEffectState.on then
				return
			end

			if input2.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseWheel then
				if cameraEffectState.d < 11 then
					cameraEffectState.d = 11
				end

				if input2.Position.Z > 0 then
					cameraEffectState.d = cameraEffectState.d + cameraEffectState.inc
				elseif input2.Position.Z < 0 then
					cameraEffectState.d = cameraEffectState.d - cameraEffectState.inc
				end
			end
		end)

		cameraEffectState.c = workspace.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child11)
			if not cameraEffectState.on or child11.Name ~= "GrabParts" or not child11:IsA("Model") then
				return
			end

			task.wait(0.2)
			local dragPartScroll

			if not child11.Parent then
				return
			else
				local dragPart2 = child11:FindFirstChild("DragPart")

				if not dragPart2 then
					return
				else
					dragPartScroll = dragPart2:Clone()
					dragPartScroll.Name = "DragPart_scroll"

					local dragAttach = dragPartScroll:FindFirstChild("DragAttach")

					if dragPartScroll:FindFirstChild("AlignPosition") and dragAttach then
						dragPartScroll.AlignPosition.Attachment1 = dragAttach
					end

					dragPartScroll.Parent = child11
					cameraEffectState.d = (dragPartScroll.Position - currentCamera.CFrame.Position).Magnitude

					if dragPartScroll:FindFirstChild("AlignOrientation") then
						dragPartScroll.AlignOrientation.Enabled = false
					end

					if dragPart2:FindFirstChild("AlignPosition") then
						dragPart2.AlignPosition.Enabled = false
					end

					task.spawn(function()
						while child11.Parent and cameraEffectState.on do
							dragPartScroll.Position = currentCamera.CFrame.Position
								+ currentCamera.CFrame.LookVector * cameraEffectState.d

							runService.RenderStepped:Wait()
						end

						if dragPartScroll then
							dragPartScroll:Destroy()
						end

						cameraEffectState.d = 11
					end)

					return
				end
			end
		end)
	end

	GrabLinesTab:CreateToggle({
		Name = 'Extend Line (PC)  <font face="GothamBlack" color="rgb(255, 255, 255)">(PLAYER & OBJECT)</font>    <font face="GothamBlack" color="rgb(7,255,0)">GRAB</font>   <font face="GothamBlack" color="rgb(255,60,0)">STRETCH</font>',
		CurrentValue = false,
		Flag = "extendLineToggleFlag",

			cameraEffectState.on = value113

				restoreCameraSettings()

		end,
	})

	GrabLinesTab:CreateToggle({
		Name = 'Spinning <font face="GothamBlack" color="rgb(255,0,0)">R</font><font face="GothamBlack" color="rgb(255,127,0)">A</font><font face="GothamBlack" color="rgb(255,255,0)">I</font><font face="GothamBlack" color="rgb(0,255,0)">N</font><font face="GothamBlack" color="rgb(0,255,255)">B</font><font face="GothamBlack" color="rgb(0,0,255)">O</font><font face="GothamBlack" color="rgb(255,0,255)">W</font> Line   <font face="GothamBlack" color="rgb(7,255,0)">GRAB</font>',
		CurrentValue = false,
		Flag = "SpinningRainbowLine",
		Callback = function(value118)
			spinningLine = value118

			local waitForChild6, createGrabLine, extendGrabLine, updateLineColorsEvent, huge, character208, position7, getRainbowColor

			if spinningLine then
				local character209 = players.LocalPlayer
				local character30 = character209.Character or character209.CharacterAdded:Wait()

				waitForChild6 = character30:WaitForChild("Torso", 5) or character30:WaitForChild("UpperTorso", 5)

				if not waitForChild6 then
					return
				end

				local grabEvents = replicatedStorage:WaitForChild("GrabEvents")
				createGrabLine = grabEvents:WaitForChild("CreateGrabLine")
				extendGrabLine = grabEvents:WaitForChild("ExtendGrabLine")
				updateLineColorsEvent =
					replicatedStorage:WaitForChild("DataEvents"):WaitForChild("UpdateLineColorsEvent")
				huge = math.huge
				character208 = 0
				position7 = waitForChild6.Position

				function getRainbowColor(index52)
					local connection210 = tick()
					return Color3.fromHSV((connection210 * 0.2 + index52) % 1, 1, 1)
				end

				rainbowConnection = runService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(delta2)
					if not spinningLine then
						if rainbowConnection then
							rainbowConnection:Disconnect()
						end

						return
					else
						character208 = (character208 + 2 * math.pi / 2 * delta2) % (2 * math.pi)
						local numericValue211 = 10 + math.sin(tick() * 0) * 0
						local positionValue212 = math.cos(character208)
						local positionValue213 = tick()
						local positionValue214 = math.sin(positionValue213 * 2)
						local positionValue215 = math.sin(character208)
						local vector4 = Vector3.new(positionValue212 * numericValue211, positionValue214 * 1.5, positionValue215 * numericValue211)

						position7 = position7:Lerp(waitForChild6.Position + vector4, math.clamp(delta2 * huge, 0, 1))

						createGrabLine:FireServer(waitForChild6, CFrame.new((position7 - waitForChild6.Position) * -1))

						extendGrabLine:FireServer(0)

						updateLineColorsEvent:FireServer(
							ColorSequence.new({
								ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, getRainbowColor(0)),
								ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, getRainbowColor(0.5)),
							}),
							getRainbowColor(0),
							getRainbowColor(0.125),
							getRainbowColor(0.25),
							getRainbowColor(0.375),
							getRainbowColor(0.5),
							getRainbowColor(0.625),
							getRainbowColor(0.75),
							getRainbowColor(0.875),
							getRainbowColor(0),
							getRainbowColor(0)
						)

						return
					end
				end)

				return
			end

			if rainbowConnection then
				rainbowConnection:Disconnect()
			end
		end,
	})

	lagEnabled = false
	lagIterations = 10

	function lagF()
		while lagEnabled do
			local character31 = localPlayer.Character or localPlayer.CharacterAdded:Wait()
			character31:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			character31:WaitForChild("Humanoid")

			if CreateGrabLine then
				for i8 = 0, lagIterations do
					for index53, value119 in ipairs(players:GetPlayers()) do
						if value119 ~= localPlayer and value119.Character then
							local torso2 = value119.Character:FindFirstChild("Torso")

							local rootPart216 = torso2
							rootPart216 = torso2 or value119.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

							if rootPart216 then
								CreateGrabLine:FireServer(rootPart216, rootPart216.CFrame)
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end

			task.wait(1)
		end
	end

	function DisableCollisions(p38)

				value122.CanCollide = false
			end

	function isTooHigh(p40)
		local character32 = p40.Character
		local rootPart218 = character32 and character32:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		return not rootPart218 or rootPart218.Position.Y > 100000
	end

	function getCurrentCharacterRoot()
		local character33 = localPlayer.Character

		if character33 then
			local humanoid219 = character33:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

			if humanoid219 and humanoid219.SeatPart then
				local parent4 = humanoid219.SeatPart.Parent

				if parent4 and parent4.Name == "CreatureBlobman" then
					return parent4
				end
			end
		end

		local findFirstChild9 = Workspace:FindFirstChild(localPlayer.Name .. "SpawnedInToys")

		if findFirstChild9 then
			local creatureBlobman2 = findFirstChild9:FindFirstChild("CreatureBlobman")

			if creatureBlobman2 then
				return creatureBlobman2
			end

			return nil
		end

		return nil
	end

	function isCharacterReady()
		local character34 = localPlayer.Character

		if not character34 then
			return false
		end

		return character34:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") ~= nil
	end

	function isCharacterRagdolled()
		local character220 = getCurrentCharacterRoot()

		if character220 then
			return character220
		end

		validateLoopTarget()

		for i9 = 1, 30 do
			task.wait(0.1)
			local character221 = getCurrentCharacterRoot()

			if character221 then
				return character221
			end
		end

		return nil
	end

	function validateLoopTarget()
		if not isCharacterReady() then
			return
		end

		local rootPart222 = localPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, -8, 8)

		task.spawn(function()
			pcall(function()
				replicatedStorage.MenuToys.SpawnToyRemoteFunction:InvokeServer(
					"CreatureBlobman",
					rootPart222,
					Vector3.new(0, 177.22, 0)
				)
			end)
		end)
	end

	BlobmanController = {}
	BlobmanController.__index = BlobmanController

	function BlobmanController.new()
		return setmetatable({}, BlobmanController)
	end

	function BlobmanController:attach(cframe6)
		self:detach()
		local cameraAnchor = Instance.new("Part")
		local vector5 = Vector3.new(0.2, 0.2, 0.2)
		local positionValue223 = Workspace

		cameraAnchor.Name = "CameraAnchor"
		cameraAnchor.Size = vector5
		cameraAnchor.Transparency = 1
		cameraAnchor.Anchored = true
		cameraAnchor.CanCollide = false
		cameraAnchor.CFrame = cframe6
		cameraAnchor.Parent = positionValue223

		self.part = cameraAnchor

		local currentCamera2 = Workspace.CurrentCamera
		currentCamera2.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
		currentCamera2.CameraSubject = cameraAnchor
	end

	function BlobmanController:detach()
		if self.part then
			self.part:Destroy()
			self.part = nil
		end

		local currentCamera3 = Workspace.CurrentCamera
		local character35 = localPlayer.Character

		if character35 and character35:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") then
			currentCamera3.CameraSubject = character35.Humanoid
		else
			currentCamera3.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
			currentCamera3.CameraSubject = currentCamera3
		end
	end

	blobmanController = BlobmanController.new()

	function saveOriginalPosAttr()
		local character36 = localPlayer.Character
		local rootPart224 = character36 and character36:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

		if rootPart224 then
			character36:SetAttribute("OriginalPosition", rootPart224:GetPivot())
		end
	end

	function getOriginalPosAttr()
		local character37 = localPlayer.Character
		return character37 and character37:GetAttribute("OriginalPosition") or nil
	end

	function initCharAttrs()
		local character38 = localPlayer.Character

		if character38 and character38:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
			character38:SetAttribute("OriginalPosition", character38.HumanoidRootPart:GetPivot())
			character38:SetAttribute("SavingOriginalPos", false)
		end
	end

	function scheduleReturnHome()
		local scheduleReturnHomeValue225 = getOriginalPosAttr()

		if not scheduleReturnHomeValue225 then
			return
		end

		local connect36 = nil

		connect36 = runService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
			local character39 = localPlayer.Character
			local rootPart226 = character39 and character39:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

			if rootPart226 then
				rootPart226:PivotTo(scheduleReturnHomeValue225)

				if getgenv().originalFallenHeight then
					Workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight = getgenv().originalFallenHeight
				end

				character39:SetAttribute("SavingOriginalPos", false)
			end

			blobmanController:detach()
			connect36:Disconnect()
		end)
	end

	function modifyTarget(p41, p42, p43)
		local modifyTargetValue227 = not (p41 and p42) or p42.Health <= 0
		local bodyAngularVelocity2, bodyVelocity7

		if modifyTargetValue227 then
			return
		else
			if p43 then
				local character228 = isCharacterRagdolled()

				if character228 and character228:FindFirstChild("BlobmanSeatAndOwnerScript") then
					local creatureDrop = character228.BlobmanSeatAndOwnerScript:FindFirstChild("CreatureDrop")

					if creatureDrop then
						for index56, value124 in ipairs(p42.Parent:GetDescendants()) do
							if value124:IsA("Weld") or value124:IsA("BallSocketConstraint") then
								creatureDrop:FireServer(value124, value124)
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end

			p42.Sit = false
			p42:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running)
			p42:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Seated, false)
			p42:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)

			local getPlayerFromCharacter = players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(p42.Parent)

			if getPlayerFromCharacter and getPlayerFromCharacter:FindFirstChild("IsHeld") then
				getPlayerFromCharacter.IsHeld.Value = false
			end

			local ragdolled3 = p42:FindFirstChild("Ragdolled")

			if ragdolled3 then
				ragdolled3.Value = false
			end

			bodyVelocity7 = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
			bodyAngularVelocity2 = Instance.new("BodyAngularVelocity")

			bodyVelocity7.MaxForce = Vector3.new(10000000, -10000000, 10000000)
			bodyVelocity7.P = 1000000
			bodyVelocity7.Velocity = Vector3.new(math.random(-500, 50), -50, math.random(-50, 50))

			bodyAngularVelocity2.MaxTorque = Vector3.new(-10000000, -10000000, -10000000)
			bodyAngularVelocity2.P = 1000000

			bodyAngularVelocity2.AngularVelocity =
				Vector3.new(math.random(-500, 300), math.random(-300, 300), math.random(-500, 500))

			bodyVelocity7.Parent = p41
			bodyAngularVelocity2.Parent = p41

			p42.BreakJointsOnDeath = false
			p42:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead)
			p42.RigType = Enum.HumanoidRigType.R15

			task.delay(2, function()
				if bodyVelocity7.Parent then
					bodyVelocity7:Destroy()
				end

				if bodyAngularVelocity2.Parent then
					bodyAngularVelocity2:Destroy()
				end
			end)

			return
		end
	end

	function performKill()
		if not w then
			return
		else
			local findFirstChild10 = players:FindFirstChild(w)
			local character40 = findFirstChild10 and findFirstChild10.Character
			local rootPart229 = character40 and character40:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			local rootPart230 = character40 and character40:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
			local head6 = character40 and character40:FindFirstChild("Head")

			if not (findFirstChild10 and rootPart229 and rootPart230 and head6) then
				return
			end

			if isTooHigh(findFirstChild10) then
				return
			end

			if findFirstChild10:FindFirstChild("InPlot") and findFirstChild10.InPlot.Value then
				return
			elseif rootPart230:GetState() == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead then
				return
			else
				local character41 = localPlayer.Character
				local rootPart231 = character41 and character41:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

				if not (character41 and rootPart231) then
					return
				else
					if not character41:GetAttribute("SavingOriginalPos") then
						saveOriginalPosAttr()
					end

					character41:SetAttribute("SavingOriginalPos", true)
					getgenv().originalFallenHeight = Workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight
					Workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight = (0 / 0)
					local character232 = getOriginalPosAttr()

					if character232 then
						blobmanController:attach(character232)
					end

					rootPart231:PivotTo((CFrame.new(rootPart229.Position + vector)))
					setNoCollideChar(character40)
					SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(rootPart229, rootPart229.CFrame)
					task.wait(0.05)
					DestroyGrabLine:FireServer(rootPart229)
					task.wait(0.05)

					if head6:FindFirstChild("PartOwner") and head6.PartOwner.Value == localPlayer.Name then
						task.wait(0.05)
						modifyTarget(rootPart229, rootPart230, false)
					end

					scheduleReturnHome()
					return
				end
			end
		end
	end

	function StartLoopKill()
		if connect18 then
			connect18:Disconnect()
		end

		connect18 = runService.Heartbeat:Connect(performKill)
	end

	function StopLoopKill()
		if connect18 then
			connect18:Disconnect()
			connect18 = nil
		end

		blobmanController:detach()
	end

	function sendToSky(parent5, p44)
		DisableCollisions(p44.Parent)

		local bodyVelocity8 = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
		bodyVelocity8.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, 1000000000, 0)
		bodyVelocity8.MaxForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
		bodyVelocity8.P = 100000000
		bodyVelocity8.Parent = parent5

		p44.Sit = false
		p44.Jump = true

		task.delay(3, function()
			if bodyVelocity8.Parent then
				bodyVelocity8:Destroy()
			end
		end)
	end

	function executeKick()
		if not w then
			return
		else
			local findFirstChild11 = players:FindFirstChild(w)
			local character42 = findFirstChild11 and findFirstChild11.Character
			local rootPart233 = character42 and character42:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			local head7 = character42 and character42:FindFirstChild("Head")
			local rootPart234 = character42 and character42:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")

			if not (rootPart233 and head7 and rootPart234) or rootPart234.Health <= 0 then
				return
			end

			if isTooHigh(findFirstChild11) then
				return
			end

			if findFirstChild11:FindFirstChild("InPlot") and findFirstChild11.InPlot.Value then
				return
			else
				local character43 = localPlayer.Character

				if not (character43 and character43:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")) then
					return
				else
					local getPivot = character43:GetPivot()
					character43:PivotTo(CFrame.new(rootPart233.Position + Vector3.new(0, 0, -3)))
					DisableCollisions(character42)
					SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(rootPart233, rootPart233.CFrame)
					task.wait()
					character43:PivotTo(getPivot)
					task.wait(0.1)
					DestroyGrabLine:FireServer(rootPart233)
					task.wait(0.1)
					local partOwner4 = head7:FindFirstChild("PartOwner")

					if partOwner4 and partOwner4.Value == localPlayer.Name then
						sendToSky(rootPart233, rootPart234)
					end

					return
				end
			end
		end
	end

	function StartLoopKick()
		if connect19 then
			connect19:Disconnect()
		end

		disableModeEnabled = true

		connect19 = runService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
			if disableModeEnabled then
				executeKick()
			end
		end)
	end

	function StopLoopKick()
		disableModeEnabled = false

		if connect19 then
			connect19:Disconnect()
			connect19 = nil
		end
	end

	cframe = nil

	LoopFling = {
		on = false,
		targets = {},
		tpSpeed = 0.3,
		magnetRange = 5,
		stay = 0.5,
		back = 0.5,
	}

	function tpTo(p45)
		local character44 = localPlayer.Character
		local rootPart235 = character44 and character44:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

		if not rootPart235 then
			return
		end

		for i10 = 1, 5 do
			rootPart235.CFrame = rootPart235.CFrame:Lerp(p45, LoopFling.tpSpeed)
			task.wait(0.02)
		end
	end

	function stick(index57)
		local character45 = localPlayer.Character
		local rootPart236 = character45 and character45:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

		if not rootPart236 then
			return
		end

		if (rootPart236.Position - index57.Position).Magnitude < LoopFling.magnetRange then
			rootPart236.CFrame = index57.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 0, -1)
			rootPart236.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
		end
	end

	function execFling(p46)
		local rootPart237 = p46:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		local rootPart238 = p46:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

		if not (rootPart237 and rootPart238) then
			return
		else
			SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(rootPart238, rootPart238.CFrame)

			local bodyVelocity9 = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
			bodyVelocity9.MaxForce = Vector3.new(100000, 100000, 100000)
			bodyVelocity9.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, 1000, 0)
			bodyVelocity9.Parent = rootPart238

			local positionValue239 = true

			while LoopFling.on do
				bodyVelocity9.Velocity = positionValue239 and Vector3.new(0, 1000, 0) or Vector3.new(0, -1000, 0)
				positionValue239 = not positionValue239
				task.wait(0.5)
			end

			bodyVelocity9:Destroy()
			return
		end
	end

	function startFlingFor(p47)
		if not p47 then
			return
		end

		while LoopFling.on do
			local character46 = p47.Character
			local rootPart240 = character46 and character46:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

			if rootPart240 then
				tpTo(rootPart240.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 0, -2))
				task.wait(0.1)
				stick(rootPart240)
				execFling(character46)
				task.wait(LoopFling.stay)
			end

			if cframe then
				tpTo(cframe)
				task.wait(LoopFling.back)
			end

			task.wait()
		end
	end

	function autoFlingLoop()
		while LoopFling.on do
			for index58, value125 in ipairs(LoopFling.targets) do
				local findFirstChild12 = players:FindFirstChild(value125)

				if findFirstChild12 and findFirstChild12.Character then
					task.spawn(function()
						startFlingFor(findFirstChild12)
					end)
				end
			end

			task.wait(0.1)
		end
	end

	function StartLoopBounce(p48)
		if not p48 or p48 == "" then
			return
		else
			local character47 = localPlayer.Character
			local rootPart241 = character47 and character47:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

			if rootPart241 then
				cframe = rootPart241.CFrame
			end

			LoopFling.on = true
			LoopFling.targets = { p48 }

			task.spawn(autoFlingLoop)
			return
		end
	end

	function StopLoopBounce()
		LoopFling.on = false
		local rootPart242 = cframe
		cframe = nil
		task.wait(0.1)
		local character48 = localPlayer.Character
		local rootPart243 = character48 and character48:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

		if rootPart243 and rootPart242 then
			rootPart243.CFrame = rootPart242
		end
	end

	function LoopGetRole(p49)
		local playerRole244 = string.lower(p49)

		if superAdmins[playerRole244] then
			return "superadmin"
		end

		if admins[playerRole244] then
			return "admin"
		end

		return "user"
	end

	function LoopOptionText(p50)
		return string.format("👤 @%s (%s)", p50.Name, p50.DisplayName or "")
	end

	function LoopRefreshDropdown()
		local playerRole245 = {}

		for index59, value127 in ipairs(players:GetPlayers()) do
			if value127 ~= localPlayer then
				if LoopGetRole(value127.Name) ~= "superadmin" then
					table.insert(playerRole245, LoopOptionText(value127))
				end
			end
		end

		LoopPlayerDropdown:Refresh(playerRole245)
	end

	players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function()
		task.wait(0.5)
		LoopRefreshDropdown()
	end)

	players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function()
		task.wait(0.5)
		LoopRefreshDropdown()
	end)

							or count2 >= 10
					until partOwner5

					head8.CFrame = CFrame.new(cframe7.Position + cframe7.LookVector * 5)
					replicatedStorage:WaitForChild("GrabEvents"):WaitForChild("DestroyGrabLine"):FireServer(head8)

	function runBlobmanAction(p51)
		local transform248 = players.LocalPlayer
		local transform249, transform250, cframe8, transform251, vehicleSeat2, creatureDrop2

		if not transform248 then
			return
		else
			local character49 = transform248.Character

			if not character49 then
				return
			else
				transform249 = character49:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
				local rootPart252 = character49:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

				if not transform249 or not rootPart252 then
					return
				else
					local findFirstChild14 = players:FindFirstChild(p51)

					if not findFirstChild14 or not findFirstChild14.Character then
						return
					end

					transform250 = findFirstChild14.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

					if not transform250 then
						return
					else
						cframe8 = rootPart252.CFrame
						local character253 = tick()
						transform251 = getCurrentCharacterRoot()

						if not transform251 then
							isCharacterRagdolled()
							task.wait(0.6)
							transform251 = getCurrentCharacterRoot()
						end

						if not transform251 then
							validateLoopTarget()
							task.wait(1.7)
							transform251 = getCurrentCharacterRoot()
						end

						if not transform251 or not transform251.PrimaryPart then
							return
						end

						vehicleSeat2 = transform251:FindFirstChild("VehicleSeat")

						if not vehicleSeat2 then
							return
						end

						pcall(function()
							vehicleSeat2:Sit(transform249)
						end)
						task.wait(0.4)

						if transform249.SeatPart ~= vehicleSeat2 then
							return
						else
							local character254 = false
							local blobmanSeatAndOwnerScript = transform251:FindFirstChild("BlobmanSeatAndOwnerScript")

							while tick() - character253 < 10 do
								local character255 = not findFirstChild14.Character
								local character256 = character255

								if not character255 then
									character255 = not transform250
									character256 = character255
								end

								if character256 then
									break
								else
									pcall(function()
										transform251:SetPrimaryPartCFrame(transform250.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, -4))
									end)

									local leftDetector = transform251:FindFirstChild("LeftDetector")
										or transform251:FindFirstChild("RightDetector")

									local leftWeld = leftDetector
										and (
											leftDetector:FindFirstChild("LeftWeld")
											or leftDetector:FindFirstChild("RightWeld")
										)

									if leftDetector and leftWeld and blobmanSeatAndOwnerScript then
										local creatureGrab = blobmanSeatAndOwnerScript:FindFirstChild("CreatureGrab")

										if creatureGrab then
											pcall(function()
												creatureGrab:FireServer(leftDetector, transform250, leftWeld)
											end)
										end
									end

									if leftWeld and leftWeld.Attachment0 and leftWeld.Attachment0.Parent == transform250 then
										character254 = true
										break
									end

									task.wait(0.1)
								end
							end

							if not character254 then
								rayfield:Notify({
									Title = "Error",
									Content = "Cannot Grab the person.",
									Duration = 5,
									Image = "x",
								})

								pcall(function()
									replicatedStorage.MenuToys.DestroyToy:FireServer(transform251)
								end)
								rootPart252.CFrame = cframe8
								return
							end

							pcall(function()
								transform250.CFrame = CFrame.new(cframe8.Position + cframe8.LookVector * 5)
							end)

							rootPart252.CFrame = cframe8
							task.wait(0.4)

							if blobmanSeatAndOwnerScript then
								creatureDrop2 = blobmanSeatAndOwnerScript:FindFirstChild("CreatureDrop")
								local leftDetector2 = transform251:FindFirstChild("LeftDetector")

								local rightDetector = leftDetector2
								rightDetector = leftDetector2 or transform251:FindFirstChild("RightDetector")

								local leftWeld2 = rightDetector
									and (
										rightDetector:FindFirstChild("LeftWeld")
										or rightDetector:FindFirstChild("RightWeld")
									)

								if creatureDrop2 and leftWeld2 then
									pcall(function()
										creatureDrop2:FireServer(leftWeld2)
									end)
								end
							end

							task.delay(0.25, function()
								pcall(function()
									replicatedStorage.MenuToys.DestroyToy:FireServer(transform251)
								end)
							end)

							return
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end

	function runBlobmanLoopAction()
		local transform260, transform261, transform262, transform263, cframe9, parent6, vehicleSeat3, cframe10, leftDetector3, creatureGrab2, creatureRelease

		if not loopKillEnabled then
			return
		elseif not w then
			return
		else
			local character264 = players.LocalPlayer

			if not character264 or not character264.Character then
				return
			else
				local character50 = character264.Character
				transform260 = character50:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
				transform261 = character50:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

				if not transform260 or not transform261 then
					return
				else
					local findFirstChild17 = players:FindFirstChild(w)

					if not findFirstChild17 or not findFirstChild17.Character then
						return
					end

					transform262 = findFirstChild17.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
					transform263 = findFirstChild17.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

					if not transform262 or not transform263 or transform262.Health <= 0 then
						return
					end

					if transform263.Position.Y > 100000 then
						return
					end

					cframe9 = transform261.CFrame
					parent6 = transform260.SeatPart and transform260.SeatPart.Parent

					if not parent6 or parent6.Name ~= "CreatureBlobman" then
						parent6 = getCurrentCharacterRoot()

						if not parent6 then
							validateLoopTarget()
							task.wait(1.6)
							parent6 = getCurrentCharacterRoot()
						end

						if not parent6 or not parent6.PrimaryPart then
							return
						end

						vehicleSeat3 = parent6:FindFirstChild("VehicleSeat")

						if not vehicleSeat3 then
							return
						end

						pcall(function()
							vehicleSeat3:Sit(transform260)
						end)
						task.wait(0.35)

						if transform260.SeatPart ~= vehicleSeat3 then
							return
						end
					end

					if not parent6 or not parent6.PrimaryPart then
						return
					else
						cframe10 = parent6.PrimaryPart.CFrame
						task.wait(0.15)

						leftDetector3 = parent6:FindFirstChild("LeftDetector")
							or parent6:FindFirstChild("RightDetector")

						local leftWeld3 = leftDetector3
							and (leftDetector3:FindFirstChild("LeftWeld") or leftDetector3:FindFirstChild("RightWeld"))

						local blobmanSeatAndOwnerScript2 = parent6:FindFirstChild("BlobmanSeatAndOwnerScript")

						creatureGrab2 = blobmanSeatAndOwnerScript2
							and blobmanSeatAndOwnerScript2:FindFirstChild("CreatureGrab")

						creatureRelease = blobmanSeatAndOwnerScript2
							and blobmanSeatAndOwnerScript2:FindFirstChild("CreatureRelease")

						if leftDetector3 and leftWeld3 and creatureGrab2 and creatureRelease then
							for i11 = 1, 4 do
								if not loopKillEnabled then
									break
								end

								if not findFirstChild17.Character then
									break
								end

								pcall(function()
									creatureGrab2:FireServer(leftDetector3, transform263, leftWeld3)
								end)
								task.wait(0.05)

								pcall(function()
									transform262.RigType = Enum.HumanoidRigType.R15
									transform262.PlatformStand = true
									transform262:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics)
									transform262:TakeDamage(100)
									transform262.Health = 0
								end)

								task.wait(0.05)
								task.wait(0.05)

								pcall(function()
									creatureRelease:FireServer(leftWeld3, transform263)
								end)
								task.wait(0.08)
							end
						end

						task.wait(0.1)
