	LoopTab:CreateLabel("Utility", "settings")

	AutoFreezeBlobmanEnabled = false
	FreezedBlobs = {}
	trackedBlobs = {}

	function freezeBlobman(key21)
		if not key21 or not key21.PrimaryPart then
			return
		elseif FreezedBlobs[key21] then
			return
		else
			FreezedBlobs[key21] = true

			local autoFreezeVelocity = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
			autoFreezeVelocity.Name = "AutoFreezeVelocity"
			autoFreezeVelocity.MaxForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
			autoFreezeVelocity.Velocity = Vector3.zero
			autoFreezeVelocity.P = 10000
			autoFreezeVelocity.Parent = key21.PrimaryPart

			local autoFreezeGyro = Instance.new("BodyGyro")
			autoFreezeGyro.Name = "AutoFreezeGyro"
			autoFreezeGyro.MaxTorque = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
			autoFreezeGyro.P = 10000
			autoFreezeGyro.D = 500
			autoFreezeGyro.CFrame = key21.PrimaryPart.CFrame
			autoFreezeGyro.Parent = key21.PrimaryPart

			return
		end
	end

	function unfreezeBlobman(key22)
		if not key22 or not key22.PrimaryPart then
			return
		end

		FreezedBlobs[key22] = nil

		pcall(function()
			key22.PrimaryPart:FindFirstChild("AutoFreezeVelocity"):Destroy()
			key22.PrimaryPart:FindFirstChild("AutoFreezeGyro"):Destroy()
		end)
	end

	function getPlayerSeatBlob()
		local character58 = localPlayer.Character
		local humanoid286 = character58 and character58:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		return humanoid286 and humanoid286.SeatPart and humanoid286.SeatPart.Parent
	end

						end

					end

		end

	function attachCameraToSpawn()
		local currentCamera5 = Workspace.CurrentCamera
		currentCamera5.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Scriptable
		currentCamera5.CFrame = CFrame.new(0, 50, 50) * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(-45), 0, 0)
	end

	function detachCamera()
		local currentCamera6 = Workspace.CurrentCamera
		local character59 = localPlayer.Character

		if character59 and character59:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") then
			currentCamera6.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
			currentCamera6.CameraSubject = character59.Humanoid
		else
			currentCamera6.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
		end
	end

	function performKillAll()
		attachCameraToSpawn()

		for index64, value146 in ipairs(players:GetPlayers()) do
			if value146 ~= localPlayer then
				if not isPlayerWhitelisted(value146) then
					local character60 = value146.Character
					local rootPart288 = character60 and character60:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
					local rootPart289 = character60 and character60:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
					local head10 = character60 and character60:FindFirstChild("Head")

					if rootPart288 and rootPart289 and head10 then
						if not isTooHigh(value146) then
							if not (value146:FindFirstChild("InPlot") and value146.InPlot.Value) then
								if rootPart289:GetState() ~= Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead then
									local character61 = localPlayer.Character
									local rootPart290 = character61 and character61:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

									if character61 and rootPart290 then
										if not character61:GetAttribute("SavingOriginalPos") then
											saveOriginalPosAttr()
										end

										character61:SetAttribute("SavingOriginalPos", true)
										Workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight = (0 / 0)
										rootPart290:PivotTo((CFrame.new(rootPart288.Position + vector)))
										setNoCollideChar(character60)
										SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(rootPart288, rootPart288.CFrame)
										task.wait(0.05)
										DestroyGrabLine:FireServer(rootPart288)
										task.wait(0.05)

										if head10:FindFirstChild("PartOwner") and head10.PartOwner.Value == localPlayer.Name then
											task.wait(0.05)
											modifyTarget(rootPart288, rootPart289)
										end
									end
								end
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end

		task.wait(0.05)
	end

	function StartLoopKillAll()
		if connect21 then
			connect21:Disconnect()
		end

		connect21 = runService.Heartbeat:Connect(performKillAll)
	end

	function StopLoopKillAll()
		if connect21 then
			connect21:Disconnect()
			connect21 = nil
		end

		detachCamera()
	end

	function executeKickAll()
		attachCameraToSpawn()

		for index65, value147 in ipairs(players:GetPlayers()) do
			if value147 ~= localPlayer then
				if not isPlayerWhitelisted(value147) then
					local character62 = value147.Character
					local rootPart291 = character62 and character62:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
					local head11 = character62 and character62:FindFirstChild("Head")
					local rootPart292 = character62 and character62:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")

					if not (not (rootPart291 and head11 and rootPart292) or rootPart292.Health <= 0) then
						if not isTooHigh(value147) then
							if not (value147:FindFirstChild("InPlot") and value147.InPlot.Value) then
								local character63 = localPlayer.Character

								if character63 and character63:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
									local getPivot2 = character63:GetPivot()
									character63:PivotTo(CFrame.new(rootPart291.Position + Vector3.new(0, 0, -3)))
									DisableCollisions(character62)
									SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(rootPart291, rootPart291.CFrame)
									task.wait()
									character63:PivotTo(getPivot2)
									task.wait(0.05)
									DestroyGrabLine:FireServer(rootPart291)
									task.wait(0.05)
									local partOwner6 = head11:FindFirstChild("PartOwner")

									if partOwner6 and partOwner6.Value == localPlayer.Name then
										sendToSky(rootPart291, rootPart292)
									end
								end
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end

		task.wait(0.05)
	end

	function StartLoopKickAll()
		if connect22 then
			connect22:Disconnect()
		end

		cameraSpawnAttached = true

		connect22 = runService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
			if cameraSpawnAttached then
				executeKickAll()
			end
		end)
	end

	function StopLoopKickAll()
		cameraSpawnAttached = false

		if connect22 then
			connect22:Disconnect()
			connect22 = nil
		end

		detachCamera()
	end

	function getRoot()
		return localPlayer.Character and localPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	end

	function getPlayerLegs(p53)
		if not p53 or not p53.Character then
			return {}
		else
			local character293 = {}

			for key25, value151 in pairs({ "Right Leg", "Left Leg" }) do
				local findFirstChild23 = p53.Character:FindFirstChild(value151)

				if findFirstChild23 and findFirstChild23:IsA("BasePart") then
					table.insert(character293, findFirstChild23)
				end
			end

			return character293
		end
	end

	function setRagdollOwner(p54)
		if p54 and p54.Parent then
			pcall(function()
				for key26, value152 in pairs(p54:GetDescendants()) do
					local childCollection294 = value152

					if childCollection294:IsA("BasePart") then
						SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(childCollection294, childCollection294.CFrame)
						pcall(function()
							DestroyGrabLine:FireServer(childCollection294)
						end)
					end
				end
			end)
		end
	end

	function spawnRagdollFood()
		if not ragdollToggleActive then
			return
		elseif tick() - ragdollLastSpawn < ragdollSpawnCooldown then
			return
		else
			ragdollLastSpawn = tick()
			local timestamp295 = getRoot()

			if not timestamp295 then
				return
			else
				local cframe18 = CFrame.new(0, 0, 10)
				transform2443 = { "FoodBanana", timestamp295.CFrame * cframe18, Vector3.new(0, 97.69, 0) }
				return
			end
		end
	end

	function handleRagdollToggle(p55)
		if not ragdollToggleActive then
			return
		end

		local waitForChild8 = p55:WaitForChild("HoldPart", 3)
		local transform296, cframe19

		if not waitForChild8 then
			return
		else
			pcall(function()
				waitForChild8.HoldItemRemoteFunction:InvokeServer(p55, localPlayer.Character)
			end)
			local rigidConstraint = waitForChild8:FindFirstChild("RigidConstraint")

			if
				rigidConstraint
				and rigidConstraint.Attachment1
				and rigidConstraint.Attachment1:IsDescendantOf(localPlayer.Character)
			then
				if not waitForChild8:FindFirstChild("EatingSound") or not waitForChild8.EatingSound.IsPlaying then
					pcall(function()
						replicatedStorage.HoldEvents.Use:FireServer(p55)
					end)
					task.wait(2.5)
				end
			end

			transform296 = {
				p55,
				getRoot().CFrame * CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(45), 0) * CFrame.new(0, -2, 3),
				Vector3.new(0, 97.69, 0),
			}

			pcall(function()
				waitForChild8.DropItemRemoteFunction:InvokeServer(unpack(transform296))
			end)
			task.wait(0.5)
			setRagdollOwner(p55)
			local getPlayers = players:GetPlayers()
			local playerListItem297 = getPlayerLegs(getPlayers[math.random(2, #getPlayers)])

			cframe19 = #playerListItem297 > 0
					and playerListItem297[math.random(1, #playerListItem297)].CFrame * CFrame.new(math.random(-2, 2), -3, math.random(-2, 2))
				or getRoot().CFrame * CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(45), 0) * CFrame.new(0, -2, 3)

			pcall(function()
				for key27, value153 in pairs(p55:GetDescendants()) do
					if value153:IsA("BasePart") then
						value153.CFrame = cframe19
					end
				end
			end)

			for key28, value154 in pairs(p55:GetChildren()) do
				if value154.Name == "BananaPeel" and value154:FindFirstChildOfClass("TouchTransmitter") then
					value154.Size = Vector3.new(10, 10, 10)
					value154.Transparency = 1

					ragdollBananaPeel = value154
					ragdollActive = true

					task.spawn(function()
						while ragdollActive and ragdollBananaPeel and ragdollBananaPeel.Parent do
							local timestamp298 = getRoot()

							if not timestamp298 then
								waitTime(0.1)
							else
								local character299 = {}

								for key29, value155 in pairs(players:GetPlayers()) do
									if not isPlayerWhitelisted(value155) then
										if value155 ~= localPlayer and value155.Character then
											local rootPart300 = value155.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

											if rootPart300 then
												local assemblyLinearVelocity = rootPart300.AssemblyLinearVelocity

												table.insert(character299, {
													player = value155,
													distance = (timestamp298.Position - rootPart300.Position).Magnitude,
													legs = getPlayerLegs(value155),
													velocity = assemblyLinearVelocity,
													speed = assemblyLinearVelocity.Magnitude,
												})
											end
										end
									end
								end

								table.sort(character299, function(p56, p57)
									return p56.distance < p57.distance
								end)

								for index66, value156 in ipairs(character299) do
									if not ragdollActive or not ragdollBananaPeel or not ragdollBananaPeel.Parent then
										break
									elseif #value156.legs > 0 then
										local numericValue301 = math.random(1, #value156.legs)
										local numericValue302 = math.random(-5, 5)
										local transform303 = math.random(-20, -10)
										local transform304 = math.random(-5, 5)
										local transform305 = tick() % 360 * 50

										ragdollBananaPeel.CFrame = value156.legs[numericValue301].CFrame
											* CFrame.new(numericValue302 / 10, transform303 / 10, transform304 / 10)
											* CFrame.Angles(math.rad(transform305), math.rad(transform305 * 0.5), 0)

										ragdollBananaPeel.AssemblyLinearVelocity = value156.velocity * 0.8
											+ Vector3.new(
												math.random(-10, 10),
												math.random(10, 30),
												math.random(-10, 10)
											)

										waitTime(0.16)
									end
								end
							end
						end
					end)

					break
				end
			end

			return
		end
	end

	function getRootVomit(p58)
		return p58 and p58:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	end

	function focusPlayerVomit(p59)
		if isPlayerWhitelisted(p59) then
			return
		else
			local character306 = getRootVomit(localPlayer.Character)

			if not character306 or not p59.Character then
				return
			else
				local humanoid307 = getRootVomit(p59.Character)
				local character64 = p59.Character
				local humanoid308 = not humanoid307
				local humanoid309 = character64:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

				if humanoid308 or humanoid309 and humanoid309.Health <= 0 then
					return
				else
					local timestamp310 = tick()
					tick()

					repeat
						if not humanoid307 or not character306 then
							break
						else
							local cframe20 = CFrame.new(0, 0.5, 3)
							character306.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt((humanoid307.CFrame * cframe20).Position, humanoid307.Position)
							Camera.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(Camera.CFrame.Position, humanoid307.Position)

							character306.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
							character306.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero

							runService.Heartbeat:Wait()
						end
					until tick() - timestamp310 >= 0.142

					return
				end
			end
		end
	end

	function tpToEveryoneVomit()
		local character311 = {}

		for index67, value158 in ipairs((players:GetPlayers())) do
			if value158 ~= localPlayer and value158.Character and not isPlayerWhitelisted(value158) then
				table.insert(character311, value158)
			end
		end

		if vomitReverseOrder then
			for i15 = #character311, 1, -1 do
				focusPlayerVomit(character311[i15])
			end
		else
			for index68, value159 in ipairs(character311) do
				focusPlayerVomit(value159)
			end
		end

		vomitReverseOrder = not vomitReverseOrder
	end

	function spawnVomitPoop()
		local character312 = getRootVomit(localPlayer.Character)

		if not character312 then
			return
		else
			local cframe21 = CFrame.new(0, 0, 10)
			transform2545 = { "PoopPile", character312.CFrame * cframe21, Vector3.new(0, 97.69, 0) }
			return
		end
	end

	end

	function spawnCampfireToy()
		local timestamp313

		if not campfireToggleActive then
			return
		elseif tick() - campfireLastSpawn < campfireSpawnCooldown then
			return
		else
			campfireLastSpawn = tick()
			local timestamp314 = getRoot()

			if not timestamp314 then
				return
			end

			timestamp313 = { "Campfire", timestamp314.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, 10), Vector3.new(0, 97.69, 0) }

			pcall(function()
				replicatedStorage.MenuToys.SpawnToyRemoteFunction:InvokeServer(unpack(timestamp313))
			end)

			return
		end
	end

	function checkCampfireFireEnabled(p62)
		local firePlayerPart2 = p62:FindFirstChild("FirePlayerPart")

		if not firePlayerPart2 then
			return false
		else
			local attachment = firePlayerPart2:FindFirstChild("Attachment")

			if not attachment then
				return false
			else
				local fireParticleEmitter = attachment:FindFirstChild("FireParticleEmitter")
				return fireParticleEmitter and fireParticleEmitter.Enabled
			end
		end
	end

	function playerHasFireInHRP(p63)
		if not p63 or not p63.Character then
			return false
		else
			local rootPart315 = p63.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

			if not rootPart315 then
				return false
			end

			for key31, value162 in pairs(rootPart315:GetDescendants()) do
				if value162:IsA("FireParticleEmitter") then
					return true
				end
			end

			return false
		end
	end

	function campfireLoop()
		task.spawn(function()
			while campfireToyActive and campfireCurrentToy and campfireCurrentToy.Parent do
				if not checkCampfireFireEnabled(campfireCurrentToy) then
					pcall(function()
						DeleteToyRE:FireServer(campfireCurrentToy)
					end)
					campfireCurrentToy = nil
					campfireToyActive = false
					spawnCampfireToy()
					waitTime(0.5)
					break
				elseif not getRoot() then
					waitTime(0.1)
				else
					local playerListItem316 = {}
					local character317 = {}

					for key32, value163 in pairs(players:GetPlayers()) do
						if not isPlayerWhitelisted(value163) then
							if value163 ~= localPlayer and value163.Character then
								if value163.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
									if playerHasFireInHRP(value163) then
										table.insert(playerListItem316, value163)
									else
										table.insert(character317, value163)
									end
								end
							end
						end
					end

					for key33, value164 in pairs(character317) do
						if not campfireToyActive or not campfireCurrentToy or not campfireCurrentToy.Parent then
							break
						else
							local rootPart318 = value164.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

							if rootPart318 then
								pcall(function()
									campfireCurrentToy:PivotTo(rootPart318.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, -2, 0))
								end)

								waitTime(0.2)
							end
						end
					end

					for key34, value165 in pairs(playerListItem316) do
						if not campfireToyActive or not campfireCurrentToy or not campfireCurrentToy.Parent then
							break
						else
							local rootPart319 = value165.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

							if rootPart319 then
								pcall(function()
									campfireCurrentToy:PivotTo(rootPart319.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, -2, 0))
								end)

								waitTime(0.2)
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end)
	end

	function GetPlayerCFrame()
		local character65 = localPlayer.Character

		if character65 then
			return character65.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame
		end
	end

	freezecampart = Instance.new("Part", Workspace)
	freezecampart.Name = "BloodyCamera"
	freezecampart.Anchored = true
	freezecampart.CanCollide = false
	freezecampart.Transparency = 1
	freezecampart.CanQuery = false
	freezecampart.Size = Vector3.new()

	function FreezeCam(cframe22)
		freezecampart.CFrame = cframe22
		Workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Follow
		Workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject = freezecampart
	end

	function unFreezeCam()
		local character66 = localPlayer.Character
		Workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject = character66:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		Workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
	end

	function CheckPlayerBring(p64)
		if isPlayerWhitelisted(p64) then
			return false
		end

		if p64 and p64.Character then
			local rootPart320 = p64.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			local rootPart321 = p64.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

			if rootPart320 and rootPart321 then
				return rootPart321:FindFirstChild("Ragdolled") ~= nil
			end

			return false
		end

		return false
	end

	function CheckNetworkOwnerShipOnPlayer(p65)
		if
			p65.Character
			and p65.Character:FindFirstChild("Head")
			and p65.Character.Head:FindFirstChild("PartOwner")
			and p65.Character.Head.PartOwner.Value == localPlayer.Name
		then
			return true
		end

		return false
	end

	function SNOWshipOnce(p66)
		local distanceFromCharacter = localPlayer:DistanceFromCharacter(p66.Position)

		if localPlayer.Character and localPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
			if distanceFromCharacter <= 30 then
				SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(p66, CFrame.lookAt(localPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position, p66.Position))
			end
		end
	end

	function CreateBringBody(p67, p68)
		if p67:FindFirstChild("BringBody") then
			p67.BringBody.Position = p68.Position
		else
			local bringBody = Instance.new("BodyPosition", p67)
			bringBody.Name = "BringBody"
			bringBody.Position = p68.Position
			bringBody.MaxForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
			bringBody.D = 5000
			bringBody.P = 1500000
		end
	end

	function TeleportPlayer(index69)
		local character67 = localPlayer.Character

		if character67 and typeof(index69) == "CFrame" then
			local rootPart322 = character67.HumanoidRootPart
			local rootPart323 = character67:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
			rootPart322.CFrame = rootPart322.CFrame.Rotation + index69.Position

			if rootPart323.SeatPart == nil or tostring(rootPart323.SeatPart.Parent) ~= "CreatureBlobman" then
				rootPart323.Sit = false
			end
		end
	end

	ServerTab:CreateToggle({
		Name = 'Bring All   <font face="GothamBlack" color="rgb(255,50,0)">FAST</font>   <font face="GothamBlack" color="rgb(0,255,0)">GLOBAL</font>   <font face="GothamBlack" color="rgb(255,0,0)">OP</font>\t <font face="GothamBlack" color="rgb(39,245,218)">PREMIUM</font>',
		CurrentValue = false,
		Callback = function(value166)
			bringAllActive = value166

			if value166 then
				playerCFrame = GetPlayerCFrame()

				if not playerCFrame then
					return
				end

				cameraCFrame = CFrame.lookAt(playerCFrame.Position + Vector3.new(0, 5, 15), playerCFrame.Position)

				Workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame = cameraCFrame

				task.spawn(function()
					while bringAllActive do
						FreezeCam(cameraCFrame)

						for key35, value167 in pairs(players:GetPlayers()) do
							if value167 ~= localPlayer and CheckPlayerBring(value167) then
								local rootPart324 = value167.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
								local rootPart325 = value167.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

								if rootPart324 and rootPart325 and rootPart325:FindFirstChild("Ragdolled") then
									for i16 = 0, 50 do
										if not bringAllActive then
											break
										end

										SNOWshipOnce(rootPart324)

										if CheckNetworkOwnerShipOnPlayer(value167) then
											rootPart324.CFrame = playerCFrame
												* CFrame.new(math.random(-3, 3) / 10, 0, math.random(-3, 3) / 10)

											rootPart324.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
											rootPart324.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)

											for key36, value168 in pairs(rootPart324:GetChildren()) do
												if
													value168:IsA("BodyVelocity")
													or value168:IsA("BodyPosition")
													or value168:IsA("BodyAngularVelocity")
												then
													value168:Destroy()
												end
											end

											local antiFloatBody = Instance.new("BodyPosition", rootPart324)
											antiFloatBody.Name = "AntiFloatBody"

											antiFloatBody.Position = playerCFrame.Position
												+ Vector3.new(math.random(-2, 2), -2, math.random(-2, 2))

											antiFloatBody.MaxForce = Vector3.new(4000, 4000, 4000)
											antiFloatBody.P = 10000
											antiFloatBody.D = 2000

											task.spawn(function()
												task.wait(2)

												if antiFloatBody and antiFloatBody.Parent then
													antiFloatBody:Destroy()
												end
											end)

											break
										end

										task.wait()

										if rootPart324.Position.Y <= -12 then
											TeleportPlayer(CFrame.new(rootPart324.Position + Vector3.new(0, 5, -15)))
										else
											TeleportPlayer(CFrame.new(rootPart324.Position + Vector3.new(0, -10, -10)))
										end
									end
								end
							end
						end

						TeleportPlayer(CFrame.new(527, 123, -376))
						task.wait()
					end

					unFreezeCam()
					TeleportPlayer(playerCFrame)
				end)

				return
			end
		end,
	})

	AuraTab:CreateLabel("Objects & Players Auras", "user-circle")

	Bodies = {}
	GrabbedObjects = {}
	Active = false
	TornadoOffset = 10
	TornadoHeight = 5
	TornadoSpeed = 0.1

	function cleanupBodyMovers()
		for key41, value178 in pairs(Bodies) do
			if value178 and value178.Parent then
				value178:Destroy()
			end
		end

		Bodies = {}
		GrabbedObjects = {}
	end

	TornadoImpact = "Players"

	function ApplyMotion(p69, index70, p70, index71, p71)
		if not p69 or p69.Anchored then
			return
		else
			local tornadoAuraVelocity = p69:FindFirstChild("TornadoAuraVelocity")

			if not tornadoAuraVelocity then
				tornadoAuraVelocity = Instance.new("BodyPosition")
				tornadoAuraVelocity.Name = "TornadoAuraVelocity"
				tornadoAuraVelocity.MaxForce = Vector3.new(1000000, 1000000, 1000000)
				tornadoAuraVelocity.P = 50000
				tornadoAuraVelocity.D = 1250
				tornadoAuraVelocity.Parent = p69

				table.insert(Bodies, tornadoAuraVelocity)
			end

			local positionValue345 = math.cos(p70)
			local positionValue346 = math.sin(p70)
			local vector7 = Vector3.new(positionValue345 * index71, p71, positionValue346 * index71)
			tornadoAuraVelocity.Position = index70.Position + vector7
			return
		end
	end

	function GetPlayerRoot(p72)
		local character73 = p72.Character

		if character73 then
			return character73:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		end
	end

	function isPlayerPart(p73)
		for index72, value179 in ipairs(players:GetPlayers()) do
			if value179.Character and p73:IsDescendantOf(value179.Character) then
				return true
			end
		end

		return false
	end

	runService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
		if not Active then
			cleanupBodyMovers()
			return
		else
			local rootObject347 = GetPlayerRoot(localPlayer)

			if not rootObject347 then
				return
			else
				local cframe23 = rootObject347.CFrame
				local transform348 = tick() * TornadoSpeed * 2 * math.pi % (2 * math.pi)

				for key42, value180 in pairs(GrabbedObjects) do
					if value180 and value180.Parent then
						ApplyMotion(value180, cframe23, transform348, TornadoOffset, TornadoHeight)
					end
				end

				return
			end
		end
	end)

	task.spawn(function()
		while task.wait(0.3) do
			if Active then
				local positionValue349 = GetPlayerRoot(localPlayer)

				if positionValue349 then
					if TornadoImpact == "Objects" or TornadoImpact == "Player & Object" then
						for key43, value181 in pairs((workspace:GetPartBoundsInRadius(positionValue349.Position, 30))) do
							local transform350 = value181

							if transform350:IsA("BasePart") and not transform350.Anchored and not isPlayerPart(transform350) then
								pcall(function()
									SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(transform350, transform350.CFrame)
								end)

								if not table.find(GrabbedObjects, transform350) then
									table.insert(GrabbedObjects, transform350)
								end
							end
						end
					end

					if TornadoImpact == "Players" or TornadoImpact == "Player & Object" then
						for key44, value182 in pairs(players:GetPlayers()) do
							if value182 ~= localPlayer and value182.Character then
								if not isPlayerWhitelisted(value182) then
									local torso3 = value182.Character:FindFirstChild("Torso")
										or value182.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

									if torso3 and (positionValue349.Position - torso3.Position).Magnitude <= 30 then
										pcall(function()
											SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(torso3, torso3.CFrame)
										end)

										if not table.find(GrabbedObjects, torso3) then
											table.insert(GrabbedObjects, torso3)
										end
									end
								end
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end)

	AuraTab:CreateToggle({
		Name = 'Telekinesis Aura    <font face="GothamBlack" color="rgb(0,200,255)">ORBIT</font>   <font face="GothamBlack" color="rgb(7,255,0)">GRAB</font>\t <font face="GothamBlack" color="rgb(255, 255, 255)">(PLAYER & OBJECT)</font>',

	airAuraTargets = {}
	airAuraConnection = nil
	airAuraTargetType = "Players"
	CONFIG = { AURA_RADIUS = 30, SCAN_DELAY = 0.1, MAX_DISTANCE = 30 }

	function isPlayerPart(p74)
		for index73, value188 in ipairs(players:GetPlayers()) do
			if value188.Character and p74:IsDescendantOf(value188.Character) then
				return true
			end
		end

		return false
	end

	function cleanUpObjects()
		for key45, value189 in pairs(airAuraTargets) do
			if value189 and value189.Parent then
				value189:Destroy()
			end
		end

		airAuraTargets = {}
	end

	AuraTab:CreateToggle({
		Name = 'Air Suspend Aura   <font face="GothamBlack" color="rgb(120,0,255)">FLY</font>    <font face="GothamBlack" color="rgb(7,255,0)">GRAB</font>\t <font face="GothamBlack" color="rgb(255, 255, 255)">(PLAYER & OBJECT)</font>',
		CurrentValue = false,
		Flag = "AirSuspendAura",
		Callback = function(value190)
			if value190 then
				task.spawn(function()
					while task.wait(0.3) do
						if not airAuraConnection then
							break
						else
							local rootPart351 = localPlayer.Character and localPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

							if rootPart351 then
								for key46, value191 in
									pairs((workspace:GetPartBoundsInRadius(rootPart351.Position, CONFIG.AURA_RADIUS)))
								do
									local positionValue352 = value191

									if positionValue352:IsA("BasePart") and not positionValue352.Anchored and not isPlayerPart(positionValue352) then
										if airAuraTargetType == "Objects" or airAuraTargetType == "Player & Object" then
											pcall(function()
												SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(positionValue352, positionValue352.CFrame)
											end)
										end
									end
								end

								if airAuraTargetType == "Players" or airAuraTargetType == "Player & Object" then
									for key47, value192 in pairs(players:GetPlayers()) do
										if value192 ~= localPlayer and value192.Character then
											if not isPlayerWhitelisted(value192) then
												local torso4 = value192.Character:FindFirstChild("Torso")
													or value192.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

												if
													torso4
													and (rootPart351.Position - torso4.Position).Magnitude
														<= CONFIG.MAX_DISTANCE
												then
													pcall(function()
														SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(torso4, torso4.CFrame)
													end)
												end
											end
										end
									end
								end
							end
						end
					end
				end)

				airAuraConnection = coroutine.create(function()
					while task.wait(CONFIG.SCAN_DELAY) do
						local rootPart353 = localPlayer.Character and localPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

						if not rootPart353 then
							return
						end

						for key48, value193 in
							pairs((workspace:GetPartBoundsInRadius(rootPart353.Position, CONFIG.AURA_RADIUS)))
						do
							if value193:IsA("BasePart") and not value193.Anchored and not isPlayerPart(value193) then
								if airAuraTargetType == "Objects" or airAuraTargetType == "Player & Object" then
									if not airAuraTargets[value193] then
										local airSuspendBV = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
										airSuspendBV.Name = "AirSuspendBV"
										airSuspendBV.MaxForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
										airSuspendBV.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, airAuraRange, 0)
										airSuspendBV.Parent = value193

										airAuraTargets[value193] = airSuspendBV
									end
								end
							end
						end

						if airAuraTargetType == "Players" or airAuraTargetType == "Player & Object" then
							for key49, value194 in pairs(players:GetPlayers()) do
								if value194 ~= localPlayer and value194.Character then
									if not isPlayerWhitelisted(value194) then
										local torso5 = value194.Character:FindFirstChild("Torso")
											or value194.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

										if
											torso5
											and (rootPart353.Position - torso5.Position).Magnitude <= CONFIG.MAX_DISTANCE
										then
											if not airAuraTargets[value194] then
												local airSuspendBV2 = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
												airSuspendBV2.Name = "AirSuspendBV"
												airSuspendBV2.MaxForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
												airSuspendBV2.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, airAuraRange, 0)
												airSuspendBV2.Parent = torso5

												airAuraTargets[value194] = airSuspendBV2
											end
										end
									end
								end
							end
						end
					end
				end)

				coroutine.resume(airAuraConnection)
			else
				if airAuraConnection then
					coroutine.close(airAuraConnection)
					airAuraConnection = nil
				end

				cleanUpObjects()
			end
		end,
	})

	dropdown = AuraTab:CreateDropdown({
		Name = 'Impact\t  <font face="GothamBlack" color="rgb(255,40,0)">SELECT</font>',
		Options = { "Players", "Objects", "Player & Object" },
		CurrentOption = { "Players" },
		MultipleOptions = false,
		Flag = "AirSuspendImpact",

			cleanUpObjects()
			airAuraTargetType = value195[1]
		end,
	})

	AuraTab:CreateToggle({

					connect27:Disconnect()
				end

				connect27 = runService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
					local rootPart355 = localPlayer.Character and localPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

					if not rootPart355 then
						return
					end

					for key50, value198 in pairs(players:GetPlayers()) do
						if value198 ~= localPlayer and value198.Character then
							if not isPlayerWhitelisted(value198) then
								local rootPart356 = value198.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

								if rootPart356 and (rootPart355.Position - rootPart356.Position).Magnitude <= 30 then
									pcall(function()
										SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(rootPart356, rootPart356.CFrame)
									end)
									local vector8 = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)

									if teleportAuraTargetName == "Cursed Cloud" then
										vector8 = Vector3.new(900000, 100, 3000)
									elseif teleportAuraTargetName == "Spawn" then
										vector8 = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
									elseif teleportAuraTargetName == "Void" then
										vector8 = Vector3.new(0, 1000000000000, 0)
									elseif teleportAuraTargetName == "Cozy Place" then
										vector8 = Vector3.new(592, 153, -101)
									elseif teleportAuraTargetName == "Big Cave" then
										vector8 = Vector3.new(-97.15, -7.35, 549.19)
									elseif teleportAuraTargetName == "Small Cave" then
										vector8 = Vector3.new(-35.08, -7.35, -275.37)
									elseif teleportAuraTargetName == "Small Cave 2" then
										vector8 = Vector3.new(137.87, -7.35, -389.94)
									elseif teleportAuraTargetName == "Very Very Small Cave" then
										vector8 = Vector3.new(191.09, -7.35, -564.27)
									elseif teleportAuraTargetName == "Island Cave" then
										vector8 = Vector3.new(90.77, 282.19, 333.66)
									elseif teleportAuraTargetName == "Poison" then
										vector8 = Vector3.new(71.25, -68.07, 269.03)
									end

									rootPart356.CFrame = CFrame.new(vector8)
								end
							end
						end
					end
				end)
			elseif connect27 then
				connect27:Disconnect()
				connect27 = nil
			end
		end,
	})

				for childCollection361, childCollection362 in Workspace:GetDescendants() do

						childCollection362.Material = Enum.Material.Neon

	Lighting.GlobalShadows = true

			if States.BeamLights then
				if descendant5.Name == "Campfire" then
					local fireLight2 = descendant5:WaitForChild("FirePlayerPart"):WaitForChild("FireLight")
					fireLight2.Brightness = 5

				elseif descendant5.Name == "FireworkSparkler" then

						descendant5:WaitForChild("Stick"):WaitForChild("Attachment"):WaitForChild("PointLight")
					pointLight2.Brightness = 5
					pointLight2.Range = 15
					pointLight2.Shadows = true
				elseif descendant5.Name == "GrabParts" then
					local grabBeam2 = descendant5:WaitForChild("BeamPart"):WaitForChild("GrabBeam")
					grabBeam2.Brightness = 10
					grabBeam2.LightEmission = 1
					grabBeam2.LightInfluence = 0
				end
			end

			if States.Textures and descendant5.Name == "Button" then
				descendant5.Material = Enum.Material.Neon

				if descendant5.Parent.Name == "BombMissile" then
					descendant5.BrickColor = BrickColor.new(210, 0, 0)
				end

		end)

		VisualTab:CreateLabel("Player ESP", "user")

		local g = _G
		g.PlayerHighlightColor = _G.PlayerHighlightColor or Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0)

		local g2 = _G
		g2.ObjectHighlightColor = _G.ObjectHighlightColor or Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0)

		highlightSettings = {
			ph = false,
			pi = false,
			oh = false,
			own = false,
			conns = {},
		}

		highlightConnections = {}
		highlightedObjects = {}
		highlightedPlayers = {}
	end

	do
		function discAll()
			for index74, value204 in ipairs(highlightSettings.conns) do
				local connection363 = value204
				pcall(function()
					connection363:Disconnect()
				end)
			end

			table.clear(highlightSettings.conns)
		end

		function rmHighlight(key52)
			if highlightConnections[key52] then
				highlightConnections[key52]:Destroy()
				highlightConnections[key52] = nil
			end
		end

		function rmIcon(key53)
			if highlightedObjects[key53] then
				highlightedObjects[key53]:Destroy()
				highlightedObjects[key53] = nil
			end
		end

		function mkHighlight(adornee2, key54)
			rmHighlight(key54)

			local highlight2 = Instance.new("Highlight")
			highlight2.FillColor = _G.PlayerHighlightColor
			highlight2.OutlineColor = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
			highlight2.FillTransparency = 0
			highlight2.OutlineTransparency = 0
			highlight2.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
			highlight2.Adornee = adornee2
			highlight2.Parent = coreGui

			highlightConnections[key54] = highlight2
		end

		function mkIcon(p75, key55)
			if not p75 then
				return
			else
				local rootPart364 = p75:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

				if not rootPart364 then
					return
				else
					rmIcon(key55)

					local billboardGui2 = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
					billboardGui2.Adornee = rootPart364
					billboardGui2.Size = UDim2.new(0, 100, 0, 100)
					billboardGui2.AlwaysOnTop = true
					billboardGui2.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)

					local imageLabel = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
					imageLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
					imageLabel.Size = UDim2.new(0, 64, 0, 64)
					imageLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -32, 0, 0)

					imageLabel.Image = "https://www.roblox.com/headshot-thumbnail/image?userId="
						.. key55.UserId
						.. "&width=420&height=420&format=png"

					imageLabel.Parent = billboardGui2

					local textLabel4 = Instance.new("TextLabel")
					textLabel4.BackgroundTransparency = 1
					textLabel4.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 20)
					textLabel4.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, -20)
					textLabel4.Text = key55.DisplayName .. " (@" .. key55.Name .. ")"
					textLabel4.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
					textLabel4.TextStrokeTransparency = 0
					textLabel4.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
					textLabel4.TextScaled = true
					textLabel4.Parent = billboardGui2

					billboardGui2.Parent = coreGui
					highlightedObjects[key55] = billboardGui2
					return
				end
			end
		end

		function attachPlayer(p76)
			local characterAdded = p76.CharacterAdded

			table.insert(
				highlightSettings.conns,
				characterAdded:Connect(function(p77)
					task.wait(0.2)

					if highlightSettings.ph and p77 then
						mkHighlight(p77, p76)
					end

					if highlightSettings.pi and p77 then
						mkIcon(p77, p76)
					end

					local connect37

					connect37 = p77:WaitForChild("Humanoid").Died:Connect(function()
						rmHighlight(p76)
						rmIcon(p76)

						if connect37 then
							connect37:Disconnect()
						end
					end)

					table.insert(highlightSettings.conns, connect37)
				end)
			)
		end

		discAll()

		for index75, value205 in ipairs(players:GetPlayers()) do
			if value205 ~= localPlayer then
				attachPlayer(value205)
			end
		end

		local playerAdded = players.PlayerAdded
		table.insert(highlightSettings.conns, playerAdded:Connect(attachPlayer))
		local playerRemoving = players.PlayerRemoving

		table.insert(
			highlightSettings.conns,
			playerRemoving:Connect(function(p78)
				rmHighlight(p78)
				rmIcon(p78)
			end)
		)
	end

		Name = 'Player ESP    <font face="GothamBlack" color="rgb(0,255,29)">HIGHLIGHT</font>',

		Callback = function(value206)
			highlightSettings.ph = value206

			for index76, value207 in ipairs(players:GetPlayers()) do
				if value207 ~= localPlayer then

					if value206 and character74 then
						mkHighlight(character74, value207)
					else
						rmHighlight(value207)
					end
				end
			end
		end,
	})

			highlightSettings.pi = value208

			for index77, value209 in ipairs(players:GetPlayers()) do

				end
			end
		end,

	VisualTab:CreateLabel("Object ESP", "box")

	function isToysFolder(p79)
		return p79:IsA("Folder") and p79.Name:match("SpawnedInToys$")
	end

	function clearMissingOH()
		for key56, value210 in pairs(highlightedPlayers) do
			if not key56 or not key56.Parent then
				if value210 then
					value210:Destroy()
				end

				highlightedPlayers[key56] = nil
			end
		end
	end

	function addObjHighlight(key57)
		if highlightedPlayers[key57] then
			return
		else
			local highlight3 = Instance.new("Highlight")
			highlight3.FillColor = _G.ObjectHighlightColor
			highlight3.OutlineColor = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
			highlight3.FillTransparency = 0
			highlight3.OutlineTransparency = 0
			highlight3.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
			highlight3.Adornee = key57
			highlight3.Parent = coreGui

			highlightedPlayers[key57] = highlight3
			return
		end
	end

	function refreshObjects()
		clearMissingOH()

		for index78, value211 in ipairs(workspace:GetChildren()) do
			if isToysFolder(value211) then
				for index79, value212 in ipairs(value211:GetDescendants()) do
					if
						value212:IsA("Model")
						and (value212.PrimaryPart or value212:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart"))
					then
						if highlightSettings.oh then
							addObjHighlight(value212)
						elseif highlightedPlayers[value212] then
							highlightedPlayers[value212]:Destroy()
							highlightedPlayers[value212] = nil
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end

	connect28 = nil
	connect29 = nil

	function toggleObjects(p80)
		highlightSettings.oh = p80

		if not p80 then
			if connect28 then
				connect28:Disconnect()
				connect28 = nil
			end

			if connect29 then
				connect29:Disconnect()
				connect29 = nil
			end

			for key58, value213 in pairs(highlightedPlayers) do
				if value213 then
					value213:Destroy()
				end

				highlightedPlayers[key58] = nil
			end

			return
		end

		refreshObjects()

		connect28 = workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(descendant6)
			if not highlightSettings.oh then
				return
			end

			if descendant6:IsA("Model") then
				local parent10 = descendant6.Parent

				while parent10 and parent10 ~= workspace and not isToysFolder(parent10) do
					parent10 = parent10.Parent
				end

				if parent10 and isToysFolder(parent10) then
					addObjHighlight(descendant6)
				end
			end
		end)

		connect29 = workspace.DescendantRemoving:Connect(function(descendant7)
			if highlightedPlayers[descendant7] then
				highlightedPlayers[descendant7]:Destroy()
				highlightedPlayers[descendant7] = nil
			end
		end)
	end

	ownedToyFolderCache = {}

	function isOwnToysFolder(p81)
		local playerName365 = p81:IsA("Folder")
		local playerName366 = playerName365

		if playerName365 then
			local spawnedInToys = p81.Name:match("SpawnedInToys$")
			local playerName367 = spawnedInToys

			if spawnedInToys then
				local isOwnToysFolderValue368 = p81.Parent == workspace
				local find = isOwnToysFolderValue368

				if not isOwnToysFolderValue368 then
					local playerName369 = workspace

					find = p81.Parent == playerName369:FindFirstChild(localPlayer.Name)
						or tostring(p81):lower():find(localPlayer.Name:lower(), 1, true)
				end

				playerName367 = find
			end

			playerName366 = playerName367
		end

		return playerName366
	end

	function clearOwn()
		for key59, value215 in pairs(ownedToyFolderCache) do
			if value215 then
				value215:Destroy()
			end

			ownedToyFolderCache[key59] = nil
		end
	end

	function addOwn(key60)
		if ownedToyFolderCache[key60] then
			return
		else
			local highlight4 = Instance.new("Highlight")
			highlight4.FillColor = _G.ObjectHighlightColor
			highlight4.OutlineColor = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
			highlight4.FillTransparency = 0
			highlight4.OutlineTransparency = 0
			highlight4.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
			highlight4.Adornee = key60
			highlight4.Parent = coreGui

			ownedToyFolderCache[key60] = highlight4
			return
		end
	end

	function refreshOwn()
		for key61, value216 in pairs(ownedToyFolderCache) do
			if not key61 or not key61.Parent then
				if value216 then
					value216:Destroy()
				end

				ownedToyFolderCache[key61] = nil
			end
		end

		for index80, value217 in ipairs(workspace:GetChildren()) do
			if isOwnToysFolder(value217) then
				for index81, value218 in ipairs(value217:GetDescendants()) do
					if
						value218:IsA("Model")
						and (value218.PrimaryPart or value218:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart"))
					then
						addOwn(value218)
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end

	function deleteLimbs(p82)
		if not p82 then
			return
		end

		for index82, value224 in ipairs({ "Right Arm", "Left Arm", "Right Leg", "Left Leg" }) do
			local findFirstChild24 = p82:FindFirstChild(value224)

			if findFirstChild24 then
				for index83, value225 in ipairs(findFirstChild24:GetChildren()) do
					if value225:IsA("Motor6D") or value225:IsA("Weld") or value225:IsA("WeldConstraint") then
						value225:Destroy()
					end
				end

				findFirstChild24.CFrame = CFrame.new(0, -10000, 0)
			end
		end
	end

	KeybindTab:CreateKeybind({

		_G.PredictionTime = 0.4

		bombConnections = {}
		bombInstances = {}
		bombLoopEnabled = false
		w2 = nil
		bombCleanupPending = false
		bombSpawnPending = false
		bombSpawnCount = 0

		function cleanupBomb(p83, p84) end
	end

	function isValidBomb(p85)
		return p85 and p85.Parent and p85.PrimaryPart and p85:FindFirstChild("PartHitDetector")
	end

	function getPredictedCFrame(index84)
		local cframe25 = index84.CFrame

		if _G.PredictionTime <= 0 then
			return cframe25
		else
			local velocity = index84.Velocity
			local magnitude2 = velocity.Magnitude

			if magnitude2 < 1 then
				return cframe25
			else
				local transform376 = index84.Position + velocity.Unit * (magnitude2 * _G.PredictionTime)
				return CFrame.new(transform376, transform376 + cframe25.LookVector)
			end
		end
	end

	function teleportBombToTarget(p86, p87)
		if not p86 or not p86.PrimaryPart or not p87 then
			return false
		end

		return (pcall(function()
			p86:SetPrimaryPartCFrame((getPredictedCFrame(p87)))
		end))
	end

	function setupBomb(p88)
		local bombObject377 = not p88 or not p88.Parent
		local waitForChild10

		if bombObject377 then
			return false
		elseif not bombLoopEnabled then
			return false
		elseif not p88:WaitForChild("ThisToysNumber", 2) then
			cleanupBomb(p88, bombInstances)
			return false
		else
			local total = 0

			while not p88.PrimaryPart and total < 4 do
				task.wait(0.05)
				total = total + 0.05
			end

			if not p88.PrimaryPart or not p88.Parent then
				cleanupBomb(p88, bombInstances)
				return false
			end

			waitForChild10 = p88:WaitForChild("Body", 1)

			if not waitForChild10 then
				cleanupBomb(p88, bombInstances)
				return false
			end

			if
				not pcall(function()
					SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(waitForChild10, waitForChild10.CFrame)
					local waitForChild11 = waitForChild10:WaitForChild("PartOwner", 0.5)

					if not waitForChild11 or waitForChild11.Value ~= localPlayer.Name then
						return false
					end

					if character and character:FindFirstChild("Head") then
						local character378 = #bombConnections
						local character379 = character.Head.Position
							+ Vector3.new(0, 1000, 0)
							+ Vector3.new(character378 % 8 * 6, -math.floor(character378 / 8) * 6, 0)

						for index85, value227 in ipairs(p88:GetChildren()) do
							if value227:IsA("BasePart") then
								value227.CanCollide = false
							end
						end

						p88:SetPrimaryPartCFrame(CFrame.new(character379))
						task.wait(0.2)

						for index86, value228 in ipairs(p88:GetChildren()) do
							if value228:IsA("BasePart") then
								value228.Anchored = true
							end
						end
					end
				end) or not p88.Parent
			then
				cleanupBomb(p88, bombInstances)
				return false
			end

			table.insert(bombConnections, p88)
			cleanupBomb(p88, bombInstances)

			p88.AncestryChanged:Connect(function()
				if not p88.Parent then
					cleanupBomb(p88, bombConnections)
				end
			end)

			return true
		end
	end

	function setupExplosionCharacter()
		character = localPlayer.Character or localPlayer.CharacterAdded:Wait()
		selectedBombTarget = localPlayer.Name .. "SpawnedInToys"
		findFirstChild = workspace:FindFirstChild(selectedBombTarget) or workspace:WaitForChild(selectedBombTarget)
		bombConnections = {}
		bombInstances = {}

		if connect31 then
			connect31:Disconnect()
		end

		connect31 = findFirstChild.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child14)
			if not bombLoopEnabled then
				return
			end

			if child14.Name == _G.ToyToLoad then
				table.insert(bombInstances, child14)

				task.spawn(function()
					task.wait(0.15)
					setupBomb(child14)
				end)
			end
		end)

		for index87, value229 in ipairs(findFirstChild:GetChildren()) do
			local childCollection380 = value229

			if bombLoopEnabled and childCollection380.Name == _G.ToyToLoad then
				table.insert(bombInstances, childCollection380)
				task.spawn(function()
					setupBomb(childCollection380)
				end)
			end
		end
	end

	setupExplosionCharacter()

	localPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
		task.wait(0.5)
		setupExplosionCharacter()
	end)

	function spawnBombs()
		if bombCleanupPending then
			return
		end

		local cframe26, vector9

		if not character or not character:FindFirstChild("Head") then
			return
		else
			local character381 = tick()

			if character381 - bombSpawnCount < 0.3 then
				return
			else
				local connection382 = _G.MaxMissiles - (#bombConnections + #bombInstances)

				if connection382 <= 0 then
					return
				end

				bombCleanupPending = true
				bombSpawnCount = character381
				cframe26 = CFrame.new(character.Head.Position)
				vector9 = Vector3.new(0, 90, 0)

				for i21 = 1, connection382 do
					if localPlayer:FindFirstChild("CanSpawnToy") and localPlayer.CanSpawnToy.Value then
						task.spawn(function()
							pcall(function()
								spawnToyRemoteFunction2:InvokeServer(_G.ToyToLoad, cframe26, vector9)
							end)
						end)

						task.wait(0.05)
					end
				end

				task.wait(0.2)
				bombCleanupPending = false
				return
			end
		end
	end

	function explodeOnTarget(p89)
		if bombSpawnPending then
			return false
		end

		if not p89 or not p89.Character then
			return false
		end

		if not p89.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
			return false
		else
			local rootPart383 = {}

			for index88, value230 in ipairs(bombConnections) do
				if isValidBomb(value230) then
					table.insert(rootPart383, value230)
				end
			end

			if #rootPart383 < _G.MaxMissiles then
				return false
			else
				bombSpawnPending = true
				local environmentValue384 = {}

				for i22 = 1, _G.MaxMissiles do
					environmentValue384[i22] = rootPart383[i22]
				end

				bombConnections = {}
				local count7 = 0

				for index89, value231 in ipairs(environmentValue384) do
					if not p89.Character then
						break
					else
						local rootPart385 = p89.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

						if not rootPart385 or not rootPart385.Parent then
							break
						end

						if isValidBomb(value231) then
							teleportBombToTarget(value231, rootPart385)

							local partObject386 = {
								{
									Radius = 17.5,
									TimeLength = 2,
									Hitbox = value231:FindFirstChild("PartHitDetector"),
									ExplodesByFire = false,
									MaxForcePerStudSquared = 225,
									Model = value231,
									ImpactSpeed = 100,
									ExplodesByPointy = false,
									DestroysModel = false,
									PositionPart = rootPart385,
								},
								rootPart385.Position,
							}

							if pcall(function()
								bombExplode:FireServer(unpack(partObject386))
							end) then
								count7 = count7 + 1
							end
						end

						if _G.ExplosionMode == "Sequential" then
							task.wait(_G.ExplosionDelay)
						else
							task.wait(0.01)
						end
					end
				end

				task.wait(0.1)
				bombSpawnPending = false
				return count7 > 0
			end
		end
	end

	function autoSpamLoop()
		while bombLoopEnabled do
			local findFirstChild26 = w2 and players:FindFirstChild(w2)

			if findFirstChild26 and findFirstChild26.Character then
				if _G.MaxMissiles == 1 then
					if not bombCleanupPending and not bombSpawnPending then
						spawnBombs()
						task.wait(0.3)

						if #bombConnections >= 1 then
							explodeOnTarget(findFirstChild26)
						end
					end
				else
					if #bombConnections < _G.MaxMissiles and not bombCleanupPending then
						spawnBombs()
					end

					if #bombConnections >= _G.MaxMissiles and not bombSpawnPending then
						if explodeOnTarget(findFirstChild26) then
							task.wait(0.4)
						end
					end
				end
			end

			task.wait(0.1)
		end
	end

	task.spawn(function()
		while true do
			task.wait(3)

			for i23 = #bombInstances, 1, -1 do
				if not bombInstances[i23] or not bombInstances[i23].Parent then
					table.remove(bombInstances, i23)
				end
			end

			for i24 = #bombConnections, 1, -1 do
				if not isValidBomb(bombConnections[i24]) then
					table.remove(bombConnections, i24)
				end
			end
		end
	end)

	end

	MiscTab:CreateLabel("Silent Aim", "Target")
	extendGrabLineRemoteEvent = GrabEvents:WaitForChild("ExtendGrabLine")
	GrabPartsModel = replicatedFirst.GrabParts
	maxDistance = 30
	pcDistance = 19
	_G.ActualFakeGrabParts = nil
	currentGrabbedPart = nil
	connect32 = nil
	connect33 = nil
	connect34 = nil

	function CheckNetworkOwnerShipOnPart(p91, p92)
		if typeof(p91) == "Instance" and p91:FindFirstChild("PartOwner") and p91.PartOwner.Value == localPlayer.Name then
			return not p92 and true or p91.PartOwner
		end

		return false
	end

	function isPlayerCharacter(p93)
		if not p93 or not p93.Parent then
			return false
		end

		return p93.Parent:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") ~= nil
	end

	function isPlayerInPlot(p94)
		if p94 and p94:FindFirstChild("InPlot") then
			return p94.InPlot.Value == true
		end

		return false
	end

	function checkObjectInRaycast()
		local currentCamera8 = Workspace.CurrentCamera
		local position9 = currentCamera8.CFrame.Position
		local lookVector3 = currentCamera8.CFrame.LookVector

		local raycastParams = RaycastParams.new()
		raycastParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Blacklist
		raycastParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = { localPlayer.Character }

		local raycast = Workspace:Raycast(position9, lookVector3 * 30, raycastParams)

		if raycast then
			local instance2 = raycast.Instance

			if instance2:IsA("BasePart") and not isPlayerCharacter(instance2) then
				return true, (position9 - raycast.Position).Magnitude
			end

			return false, math.huge
		end

		return false, math.huge
	end

	function CleanupGrab()
		if _G.ActualFakeGrabParts then
			DestroyGrabLine:FireServer(currentGrabbedPart)

			_G.ActualFakeGrabParts:Destroy()
			_G.ActualFakeGrabParts = nil

			currentGrabbedPart = nil
		end

		if connect32 then
			connect32:Disconnect()
			connect32 = nil
		end

		if connect33 then
			connect33:Disconnect()
			connect33 = nil
		end

		Workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
		userInputService.MouseBehavior = Enum.MouseBehavior.Default
		pcall(function()
			localPlayer.PlayerScripts.CharacterAndBeamMove.GrabNotifyEvent:Fire(false)
		end)
	end

	function handleGrabAction(p95)
		local character77 = localPlayer.Character

		if _G.ActualFakeGrabParts then
			if currentGrabbedPart == p95 then
				return
			end

			CleanupGrab()
		end

		local humanoid388 = character77
			and p95
			and p95:IsA("BasePart")
			and character77:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
			and character77:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid").Health > 0

		local humanoid389, grabParts9

		if humanoid388 then
			if _G.RealGrabParts then
				return
			else
				local head13 = p95.Parent:FindFirstChild("Head")
				local humanoid390 = p95.Parent:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
				humanoid389 = head13 or p95

				if humanoid390 and humanoid390.Health < 1 then
					return
				end

				SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(humanoid389, Workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame)
				CreateGrabLine:FireServer(humanoid389)
				pcDistance = (p95.Position - Workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame.Position).Magnitude

				grabParts9 = GrabPartsModel:Clone()
				grabParts9:SetAttribute("Fake", true)
				grabParts9.Name = "GrabParts"

				local grabPart10 = grabParts9.GrabPart

				grabPart10.Color = localPlayer:FindFirstChild("BeamColor") and localPlayer.BeamColor.BallColorHolder.Value
					or Color3.new(1, 1, 1)

				local grabBeam3 = grabParts9.BeamPart.GrabBeam

				grabBeam3.Color = localPlayer:FindFirstChild("BeamColor") and localPlayer.BeamColor.ColorSequenceHolder.Color
					or ColorSequence.new(Color3.new(1, 1, 1))

				grabParts9.DragPart.Anchored = true
				grabParts9.GrabPart.WeldConstraint.Part1 = p95
				grabParts9.GrabPart.Position = p95.Position
				grabParts9.GrabPart.Anchored = false
				grabParts9.BeamPart.Anchored = true

				local grabBeam4 = grabParts9.BeamPart.GrabBeam

				grabBeam4.Attachment0 = character77:FindFirstChild("CamPart")
						and character77.CamPart:FindFirstChild("Attachment")
					or nil

				grabParts9.Parent = Workspace
				_G.ActualFakeGrabParts = grabParts9
				currentGrabbedPart = p95

				task.spawn(function()
					task.wait(0.2)

					if CheckNetworkOwnerShipOnPart(humanoid389, true) == false then
						CleanupGrab()
					end
				end)

				connect32 = userInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input3, p96)
					if not p96 and input3.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseWheel then
						if input3.Position.Z < 0 then
							pcDistance = math.clamp(pcDistance - 1, 3, 30)
						elseif input3.Position.Z > 0 then
							pcDistance = math.clamp(pcDistance + 1, 3, 30)
						end

						extendGrabLineRemoteEvent:FireServer(pcDistance)
					end
				end)

				connect33 = userInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input4, p97) end)

				task.spawn(function()
					pcall(function()
						localPlayer.PlayerScripts.CharacterAndBeamMove.GrabNotifyEvent:Fire(true)
					end)

					while grabParts9.Parent and p95:IsDescendantOf(Workspace) do
						if _G.SilentAimV2 and not isPlayerCharacter(p95) then
							break
						end

						grabParts9.DragPart.Position = Workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame.Position
							+ Workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame.LookVector * pcDistance

						if Workspace:FindFirstChild("RotateOrientPart") then
							grabParts9.DragPart.DragAttach.WorldOrientation =
								Workspace.RotateOrientPart.PartOrient.WorldOrientation
						end

						grabParts9.BeamPart.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(
							grabParts9.GrabPart.Position,
							grabParts9.DragPart.Position,
							Vector3.new(0, 0, 1)
						)

						grabParts9.BeamPart.GrabBeam.CurveSize1 = (
							grabParts9.GrabPart.Position - grabParts9.DragPart.Position
						).Magnitude * 1.5

						grabParts9.GrabPart.BeamSound.PlaybackSpeed = (
							grabParts9.GrabPart.Position - grabParts9.DragPart.Position
						).Magnitude * 1.5 * 1.5 / 2 + 2.5

						task.wait()
					end

					CleanupGrab()
				end)

				return
			end
		else
			return
		end
	end

	function getNearestPlayer()
		local playerListItem391 = maxDistance + 1
		local playerListItem392

		for key66, value238 in pairs(players:GetPlayers()) do
			if
				value238 ~= localPlayer
				and value238.Character
				and value238.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
				and localPlayer.Character
				and localPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			then
				if not isPlayerInPlot(value238) then
					local magnitude3 = (
						localPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position - value238.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position
					).Magnitude

					if magnitude3 < playerListItem391 then
						playerListItem392 = value238
						playerListItem391 = magnitude3
					end
				end
			end
		end

		return playerListItem392
	end

	function handleMouseButton1()
		if _G.ActualFakeGrabParts then
			CleanupGrab()
			return
		else
			local environmentValue393, environmentValue394 = checkObjectInRaycast()

			if _G.SilentAimV2 then
				if environmentValue393 and environmentValue394 <= 30 then
					return
				end

				local rootPart395 = getNearestPlayer()

				if rootPart395 and rootPart395.Character and rootPart395.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
					if
						(localPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position - rootPart395.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude
						<= maxDistance
					then
						local head14 = rootPart395.Character:FindFirstChild("Head")

						if head14 then
							handleGrabAction(head14)
						end
					end
				end

				return
			end

			return
		end
	end

	Hitbox = { "Head", "Torso", "Left Leg", "Right Leg" }
	toggle = false
	Distance = 30
	hookSupported = hookmetamethod and getrawmetatable
	raycastFromCam = nil

	function SilentAimFunction(p98, p99)
		if not toggle or not hookSupported or not raycastFromCam then
			return raycastFromCam(p98, p99)
		else
			local rootPart396 = nil
			local huge3 = math.huge

			for key67, value240 in pairs(players:GetPlayers()) do
				if value240 ~= localPlayer and value240.Character and value240.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
					local rootPart397 = value240.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

					if rootPart397 and rootPart397.Health > 0 then
						local rootPart398 = value240.Character.HumanoidRootPart
						local rootPart399 = { camera:WorldToScreenPoint(rootPart398.Position) }

						if rootPart399[2] then
							local magnitude4 = (localPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position - rootPart398.Position).Magnitude

							if magnitude4 < huge3 then
								huge3 = magnitude4
								rootPart396 = value240
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end

			if rootPart396 and huge3 <= Distance and rootPart396.Character then
				local character400 = math.random(1, #Hitbox)
				local findFirstChild27 = rootPart396.Character:FindFirstChild(Hitbox[character400])

				if findFirstChild27 then
					local character401 = {
						Instance = findFirstChild27,
						Position = findFirstChild27.Position,
						Normal = Vector3.new(0, 1, 0),
						Material = Enum.Material.Plastic,
					}

					return character401, findFirstChild27.CFrame:ToObjectSpace(CFrame.new(character401.Position))
				end

				return raycastFromCam(p98, p99)
			end

			return raycastFromCam(p98, p99)
		end
	end

	PlayerScripts = localPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerScripts", 5)
	raycastModule = PlayerScripts and PlayerScripts:FindFirstChild("RaycastModule")

	if hookSupported and raycastModule then
		module = require(raycastModule)

		if module and module.RaycastFromCam then
			raycastFromCam = module.RaycastFromCam

			function module.RaycastFromCam(p100, p101)
				return SilentAimFunction(p100, p101)
			end
		end
	end

	function GetPlayerCharacter()

			return localPlayer.Character

	function TeleportPlayer(cframe27)
		local rootPart402 = GetPlayerCharacter()

		if rootPart402 then
			rootPart402.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = cframe27
		end
	end

	function lookAt(p102, p103)
		local unit = (p103 - p102).Unit
		local cross = unit:Cross(Vector3.new(0, 1, 0))
		local cross2 = cross:Cross(unit)
		return CFrame.fromMatrix(p102, cross, cross2)
	end

	function SNOWship(p104)
		if p104 then
			SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(p104, lookAt(localPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position, p104.Position))
		end
	end

	function areAllSlotsNeon()
		for key72, value247 in pairs(Workspace.Slots:GetChildren()) do
			if value247.SlotHandle.LightBall.Material ~= Enum.Material.Neon then
				return false
			end
		end

		return true
	end

	end

			return

			end
		end

		key74.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child15) end)
	end

	function CP()
		local part = Instance.new("Part")
		part.Anchored = true
		part.CanCollide = false
		part.Transparency = 1
		part.Size = Vector3.new(1, 1, 1)
		part.Parent = workspace

		return part
	end

	function rebuildWings()
		for index92, value259 in ipairs(spawnedToyCache) do
			if value259 and value259.Handle then
				value259.Handle:Destroy()
			end

			if value259 and value259.Segments then
				for index93, value260 in ipairs(value259.Segments) do
					if value260 then
						value260:Destroy()
					end
				end
			end
		end

		spawnedToyCache = {}
		local collection404 = PalletAmount / 2

		for i26 = 1, 2 do
			local collection405 = {}

			for i27 = 1, collection404 do
				table.insert(collection405, CP())
			end

			spawnedToyCache[i26] = { Handle = CP(), Segments = collection405 }
		end
	end

	function countAllWingPallets()
		local count8 = 0

		for index94, value261 in ipairs((getAllPalletFolders())) do
			for index95, value262 in ipairs(value261:GetChildren()) do
				if
					value262
					and (value262.Name == "WingPallet" or value262:IsA("Model") and value262.Name == "WingPallet")
				then
					count8 = count8 + 1
				end
			end
		end

		return count8
	end

	function cleanupALL()
		for index96, value263 in ipairs((getAllPalletFolders())) do
			childCollection3516 = #value263:GetChildren() - -1

			for i28 = #value263:GetChildren(), 1, -1 do
				local childCollection406 = value263:GetChildren()[i28]

				if childCollection406 and childCollection406.Name == "WingPallet" then
					pcall(function()
						DeleteToyRE:FireServer(childCollection406)
					end)
					spawnedWingCache[childCollection406] = nil
				end
			end
		end
	end

	function cleanupExcess()
		local count9 = 0

		for index97, value264 in ipairs((getAllPalletFolders())) do
			for index98, value265 in ipairs(value264:GetChildren()) do
				local childCollection407 = value265

				if childCollection407 and childCollection407.Name == "WingPallet" then
					count9 = count9 + 1

					if count9 > PalletAmount then
						pcall(function()
							DeleteToyRE:FireServer(childCollection407)
						end)
						spawnedWingCache[childCollection407] = nil
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end

	function CBM(p107)
		local wingGyro = p107:FindFirstChild("WingGyro") or Instance.new("BodyGyro")
		local wingPos = p107:FindFirstChild("WingPos") or Instance.new("BodyPosition")
		wingGyro.Name = "WingGyro"
		wingPos.Name = "WingPos"

		wingGyro.P = 15000
		wingGyro.D = 200
		wingGyro.MaxTorque = Vector3.new(1, 1, 1) * 10000000000

		wingPos.P = 15000
		wingPos.D = 200
		wingPos.MaxForce = Vector3.new(1, 1, 1) * 10000000000

		wingGyro.Parent = p107
		wingPos.Parent = p107
		return wingGyro, wingPos
	end

	function forceNetworkOwnership(key75)
		if spawnedWingCache[key75] then
			return
		end

		spawnedWingCache[key75] = true

		task.spawn(function()
			local waitForChild12 = key75:WaitForChild("SoundPart", 3)
				or key75.PrimaryPart
				or key75:FindFirstChildOfClass("BasePart")

			if not waitForChild12 then
				return
			end

			for index99, value266 in ipairs(key75:GetDescendants()) do
				if value266:IsA("BasePart") then
					value266.CanCollide = false
				end
			end

			for i29 = 1, 5 do
				SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(waitForChild12, waitForChild12.CFrame)
				task.wait(0.1)
			end
		end)
	end

	function setupAntiGrab(p108)
		local partObject408 = tick()

		task.spawn(function()
			local waitForChild13 = p108:WaitForChild("SoundPart", 3)
				or p108.PrimaryPart
				or p108:FindFirstChildOfClass("BasePart")

			if not waitForChild13 then
				return
			end

			task.wait(1.5)

			while p108.Parent do
				local autoRegrab = AutoRegrab
				local partOwner7 = waitForChild13:FindFirstChild("PartOwner")

				if
					autoRegrab
					and tick() - partObject408 > 2
					and (partOwner7 and partOwner7.Value ~= localPlayer.Name or not partOwner7)
				then
					local character78 = localPlayer.Character
					local rootPart409 = character78 and character78:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

					if rootPart409 and rootPart409.Parent then
						local cframe29 = rootPart409.CFrame
						rootPart409.CFrame = waitForChild13.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 5, 0)

						for i30 = 1, 8 do
							SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(waitForChild13, waitForChild13.CFrame)
						end

						task.wait(0.05)
						rootPart409.CFrame = cframe29
					end
				end

				task.wait(0.01)
			end
		end)
	end

	function spawnPalletsSingle(index100)
		if not canSpawn() or true then
			return
		elseif not getCurrentSpawnFolder() then
			return
		else
			local lookVector4 = workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame.LookVector
			local transform410 = index100.Position + lookVector4 * -3

			SpawnToyRF:InvokeServer("PalletLightBrown", CFrame.new(transform410, transform410 + lookVector4), Vector3.new(0, 90, 0))

			return
		end
	end

	function spawnPallets(index101)
		if not canSpawn() or spawnCooldown or true then
			return
		elseif not getCurrentSpawnFolder() then
			return
		else
			local spawnPalletsValue411 = PalletAmount - countAllWingPallets()

			if spawnPalletsValue411 <= 0 then
				return
			end

			spawnCooldown = true

			for i31 = 1, spawnPalletsValue411 do
				local transform412 = i31

				task.spawn(function()
					task.wait((transform412 - 1) * 0.05)
					local lookVector5 = workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame.LookVector
					local transform413 = index101.Position + lookVector5 * -1

					SpawnToyRF:InvokeServer(
						"PalletLightBrown",
						CFrame.new(transform413, transform413 + lookVector5),
						Vector3.new(0, 90, 0)
					)
				end)
			end

			task.delay(1, function()
				spawnCooldown = false
			end)
			return
		end
	end

	task.spawn(function()
		while true do
			task.wait(2)
		end
	end)

	function lookAt(p109, p110)
		local unit2 = (p110 - p109).Unit
		local cross3 = unit2:Cross(Vector3.new(0, 1, 0))
		local cross4 = cross3:Cross(unit2)
		return CFrame.fromMatrix(p109, cross3, cross4)
	end

	function CheckOwnership(p111)
		local partOwner8 = p111:FindFirstChild("PartOwner")
		return partOwner8 and partOwner8.Value == localPlayer.Name
	end

	function SNOWshipOnce(p112)
		if not p112 or not localPlayer.Character then
			return false
		elseif CheckOwnership(p112) then
			return true
		else
			local rootPart414 = localPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

			if rootPart414 and (rootPart414.Position - p112.Position).Magnitude <= 60 then
				SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(p112, lookAt(rootPart414.Position, p112.Position))
				return true
			end

			return false
		end
	end

	function handleDestroyGrabLine(p113)
		local rootPart415 = p113:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

		if rootPart415 then
			DestroyGrabLine:FireServer(rootPart415)
		end
	end

	function getTarget()
		local character79 = localPlayer.Character

		if not character79 then
			return nil
		else
			local currentCamera9 = Workspace.CurrentCamera

			local raycastParams2 = RaycastParams.new()
			raycastParams2.FilterDescendantsInstances = { character79 }
			raycastParams2.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude

			local raycast2 = Workspace:Raycast(
				currentCamera9.CFrame.Position,
				currentCamera9.CFrame.LookVector * 150,
				raycastParams2
			)

			if raycast2 then
				local model3 = raycast2.Instance:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")

				if model3 and model3:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") and model3 ~= character79 then
					if model3.Parent.Name == "CreatureBlobman" then
						return nil
					end

					return model3
				end

				return nil
			end

			return nil
		end
	end

	noCollisionParts = {}

	function forceNoCollision(p114)
		for key76, value268 in pairs(p114:GetDescendants()) do
			local childCollection416 = value268

			if childCollection416:IsA("BasePart") then
				childCollection416.CanCollide = false

				if not noCollisionParts[childCollection416] then
					noCollisionParts[childCollection416] = childCollection416:GetPropertyChangedSignal("CanCollide"):Connect(function()
						if _G.ControllingCreature and _G.ControlEnabled then
							childCollection416.CanCollide = false
						end
					end)
				end
			end
		end
	end

	function clearCollisionConnections()
		for key77, value269 in pairs(noCollisionParts) do
			if value269 then
				value269:Disconnect()
			end
		end

		noCollisionParts = {}
	end

	function isBlobmanPart(p115)
		if p115.Parent.Name == "CreatureBlobman" then
			return
		end

		if _G.ControllingCreature then
			handleDestroyGrabLine(_G.ControllingCreature)
			_G.ControllingCreature = nil
			task.wait(0.1)
		end

		local rootPart417 = p115:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		local rootPart418 = p115:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or p115.PrimaryPart
		local head15 = p115:FindFirstChild("Head")
		local character80 = localPlayer.Character
		local rootPart419 = character80:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		local rootPart420 = character80:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

		if not rootPart418 or not rootPart417 or not rootPart420 then
			return
		end

		_G.ControllingCreature = p115
		rootPart419.AutoRotate = false

		local instance3 = Instance.new("BodyVelocity", rootPart418)
		instance3.MaxForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, 0, math.huge)
		instance3.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)

		local instance4 = Instance.new("BodyGyro", rootPart418)
		instance4.MaxTorque = Vector3.new(0, math.huge, 0)
		instance4.P = 40000
		instance4.D = 500

		rootPart417:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Seated, false)
		rootPart417.Sit = false

		forceNoCollision(character80)

		local connect38 = character80.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(descendant9)
			if descendant9:IsA("BasePart") and _G.ControllingCreature then
				descendant9.CanCollide = false

				noCollisionParts[descendant9] = descendant9:GetPropertyChangedSignal("CanCollide"):Connect(function()
					if _G.ControllingCreature and _G.ControlEnabled then
						descendant9.CanCollide = false
					end
				end)
			end
		end)

		task.spawn(function()
			while _G.ControllingCreature == p115 and p115.Parent and _G.ControlEnabled and character80.Parent do
				local currentCamera10 = Workspace.CurrentCamera
				local moveDirection = rootPart419.MoveDirection

				rootPart420.CFrame = rootPart418.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, -10, 0)
				rootPart420.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)

				forceNoCollision(character80)
				SNOWshipOnce(head15 or rootPart418)

				if moveDirection.Magnitude > 0 then
					rootPart417:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running)
					rootPart417:Move(moveDirection, false)
					instance3.Velocity = Vector3.new(moveDirection.X, 0, moveDirection.Z) * 26
				else
					instance3.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
				end

				instance4.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(
					rootPart418.Position,
					rootPart418.Position
						+ Vector3.new(currentCamera10.CFrame.LookVector.X, 0, currentCamera10.CFrame.LookVector.Z)
				)

				currentCamera10.CameraSubject = rootPart417
				runService.Heartbeat:Wait()
			end

			handleDestroyGrabLine(p115)

			if connect38 then
				connect38:Disconnect()
			end

			clearCollisionConnections()

			if instance3 then
				instance3:Destroy()
			end

			if instance4 then
				instance4:Destroy()
			end

			rootPart419.AutoRotate = true
			Workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject = rootPart419

			rootPart420.Anchored = true
			rootPart420.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
			rootPart420.CFrame = rootPart418.CFrame * CFrame.new(10, 4, 0)

			task.wait(0.2)
			rootPart420.Anchored = false

			for key78, value270 in pairs(character80:GetDescendants()) do
				if value270:IsA("BasePart") and value270.Name ~= "CamPart" and value270.Name ~= "HumanoidRootPart" then
					value270.CanCollide = true
				end
			end
		end)
	end
