	function generateShapePath()
		local generateShapePathValue424, generateShapePathValue425, generateShapePathValue426, generateShapePathValue427, generateShapePathValue428, generateShapePathValue429, generateShapePathValue430, generateShapePathValue431, generateShapePathValue432, generateShapePathValue433, generateShapePathValue434, generateShapePathValue435

		if sparklesTargetSelected then
			return
		else
			sparklesTargetCache = {}
			local numericValue436 = sparklesInterval

			if sparklesType == "Heart" then
				for i32 = 0, 3000 do
					local numericValue437 = i32 / 3000 * math.pi * 2
					local numericValue438 = math.sin(numericValue437)
					local numericValue439 = math.cos(numericValue437)
					local positionValue440 = math.cos(2 * numericValue437)
					local positionValue441 = math.cos(3 * numericValue437)
					local positionValue442 = math.cos(4 * numericValue437)

					sparklesTargetCache[i32] = Vector3.new(
						16 * numericValue438 * numericValue438 * numericValue438 * numericValue436,
						(13 * numericValue439 - 5 * positionValue440 - 2 * positionValue441 - positionValue442) * numericValue436 + 2,
						0
					)
				end
			elseif sparklesType == "Dick" then
				for i33 = 0, 3000 do
					local numericValue443 = i33 / 3000 * math.pi * 2

					if numericValue443 < math.pi then
						generateShapePathValue434 = 4 * numericValue436 * math.cos(numericValue443)
						generateShapePathValue435 = 8 * numericValue436 + 16 * numericValue436 * math.sin(numericValue443)
					elseif numericValue443 < math.pi * 1.5 then
						local numericValue444 = (numericValue443 - math.pi) * 4
						generateShapePathValue434 = -5 * numericValue436 + 4 * numericValue436 * math.cos(numericValue444)
						generateShapePathValue435 = -2 * numericValue436 + 4 * numericValue436 * math.sin(numericValue444)
					else
						local numericValue445 = (numericValue443 - math.pi * 1.5) * 4 + math.pi
						generateShapePathValue434 = 5 * numericValue436 + 4 * numericValue436 * math.cos(numericValue445)
						generateShapePathValue435 = -2 * numericValue436 + 4 * numericValue436 * math.sin(numericValue445)
					end

					sparklesTargetCache[i33] = Vector3.new(generateShapePathValue434, generateShapePathValue435 + 2, 0)
				end
			elseif sparklesType == "L" then
				local positionValue446 = 8 * numericValue436
				local numericValue447 = 10 * numericValue436

				for i34 = 0, 3000 do
					local numericValue448 = math.abs(math.sin(i34 / 3000 * 2 * math.pi))

					if numericValue448 < 0.6 then
						generateShapePathValue432 = numericValue448 / 0.6 * positionValue446 * 2
						generateShapePathValue433 = 0
					else
						generateShapePathValue432 = positionValue446 * 2
						generateShapePathValue433 = (numericValue448 - 0.6) / 0.4 * numericValue447 * 2
					end

					sparklesTargetCache[i34] = Vector3.new(generateShapePathValue432 - positionValue446, generateShapePathValue433 + 2, 0)
				end
			elseif sparklesType == "MiddleFinger" then
				local positionValue449 = 22 * numericValue436
				local numericValue450 = 35 * numericValue436
				local numericValue451 = 14 * numericValue436

				for i35 = 0, 3000 do
					local temporaryValue452 = i35 / 3000

					if temporaryValue452 < 0.05 then
						generateShapePathValue430 = -positionValue449 * 0.5
						generateShapePathValue431 = temporaryValue452 / 0.05 * numericValue451
					elseif temporaryValue452 < 0.15 then
						local numericValue453 = (temporaryValue452 - 0.05) / 0.1 * math.pi
						generateShapePathValue430 = -positionValue449 * 0.4 + math.cos(math.pi - numericValue453) * positionValue449 * 0.1
						generateShapePathValue431 = numericValue451 + math.sin(numericValue453) * numericValue451 * 0.2
					elseif temporaryValue452 < 0.25 then
						local numericValue454 = (temporaryValue452 - 0.15) / 0.1 * math.pi
						generateShapePathValue430 = -positionValue449 * 0.2 + math.cos(math.pi - numericValue454) * positionValue449 * 0.1
						generateShapePathValue431 = numericValue451 + math.sin(numericValue454) * numericValue451 * 0.2
					elseif temporaryValue452 < 0.55 then
						local numericValue455 = (temporaryValue452 - 0.25) / 0.3

						if numericValue455 < 0.3 then
							generateShapePathValue430 = -positionValue449 * 0.1
							generateShapePathValue431 = numericValue451 + numericValue455 / 0.3 * (numericValue450 - numericValue451)
						elseif numericValue455 < 0.7 then
							local numericValue456 = (numericValue455 - 0.3) / 0.4 * math.pi
							generateShapePathValue430 = math.cos(math.pi - numericValue456) * positionValue449 * 0.1
							generateShapePathValue431 = numericValue450 + math.sin(numericValue456) * positionValue449 * 0.1
						else
							generateShapePathValue430 = positionValue449 * 0.1
							generateShapePathValue431 = numericValue450 - (numericValue455 - 0.7) / 0.3 * (numericValue450 - numericValue451)
						end
					elseif temporaryValue452 < 0.65 then
						local numericValue457 = (temporaryValue452 - 0.55) / 0.1 * math.pi
						generateShapePathValue430 = positionValue449 * 0.2 + math.cos(math.pi - numericValue457) * positionValue449 * 0.1
						generateShapePathValue431 = numericValue451 + math.sin(numericValue457) * numericValue451 * 0.2
					elseif temporaryValue452 < 0.75 then
						local numericValue458 = (temporaryValue452 - 0.65) / 0.1 * math.pi
						generateShapePathValue430 = positionValue449 * 0.4 + math.cos(math.pi - numericValue458) * positionValue449 * 0.1
						generateShapePathValue431 = numericValue451 + math.sin(numericValue458) * numericValue451 * 0.2
					elseif temporaryValue452 < 0.8 then
						generateShapePathValue430 = positionValue449 * 0.5
						generateShapePathValue431 = numericValue451 - (temporaryValue452 - 0.75) / 0.05 * numericValue451
					else
						generateShapePathValue430 = positionValue449 * 0.5 - (temporaryValue452 - 0.8) / 0.2 * positionValue449
						generateShapePathValue431 = 0
					end

					sparklesTargetCache[i35] = Vector3.new(generateShapePathValue430, generateShapePathValue431, 0)
				end
			elseif sparklesType == "Square" then
				local positionValue459 = 14 * numericValue436

				for i36 = 0, 3000 do
					local numericValue460 = i36 / 3000 * math.pi * 2
					local numericValue461 = math.floor(numericValue460 / (math.pi * 0.5)) % 4
					local numericValue462 = numericValue460 % (math.pi * 0.5) / (math.pi * 0.5) * 2 - 1

					if numericValue461 == 0 then
						generateShapePathValue429 = positionValue459
						generateShapePathValue428 = numericValue462 * positionValue459
					elseif numericValue461 == 1 then
						generateShapePathValue429 = numericValue462 * -positionValue459
						generateShapePathValue428 = positionValue459
					elseif numericValue461 == 2 then
						generateShapePathValue428 = numericValue462 * -positionValue459
						generateShapePathValue429 = -positionValue459
					else
						generateShapePathValue428 = -positionValue459
						generateShapePathValue429 = numericValue462 * positionValue459
					end

					sparklesTargetCache[i36] = Vector3.new(generateShapePathValue428, generateShapePathValue429 + 2, 0)
				end
			elseif sparklesType == "nazi cross" then
				local positionValue463 = 12 * numericValue436
				local numericValue464 = positionValue463 * 0.4
				local numericValue465 = {}

				for i37 = 0, 3000 do
					local numericValue466 = i37 / 3000 * math.pi * 2
					local numericValue467 = math.floor(numericValue466 / (math.pi * 2 / 12)) % 12
					local numericValue468 = numericValue466 % (math.pi * 2 / 12) / (math.pi * 2 / 12)

					if numericValue467 == 0 then
						generateShapePathValue426 = numericValue468 * positionValue463 * 0.5
						generateShapePathValue427 = 0
					elseif numericValue467 == 1 then
						generateShapePathValue426 = positionValue463 * 0.5
						generateShapePathValue427 = numericValue468 * numericValue464
					elseif numericValue467 == 2 then
						generateShapePathValue426 = positionValue463 * 0.5 - numericValue468 * positionValue463 * 0.5
						generateShapePathValue427 = 0
					elseif numericValue467 == 3 then
						generateShapePathValue426 = 0
						generateShapePathValue427 = -numericValue468 * positionValue463 * 0.5
					elseif numericValue467 == 4 then
						generateShapePathValue426 = numericValue468 * numericValue464
						generateShapePathValue427 = -positionValue463 * 0.5
					elseif numericValue467 == 5 then
						generateShapePathValue426 = 0
						generateShapePathValue427 = -positionValue463 * 0.5 + numericValue468 * positionValue463 * 0.5
					elseif numericValue467 == 6 then
						generateShapePathValue426 = -numericValue468 * positionValue463 * 0.5
						generateShapePathValue427 = 0
					elseif numericValue467 == 7 then
						generateShapePathValue426 = -positionValue463 * 0.5
						generateShapePathValue427 = -numericValue468 * numericValue464
					elseif numericValue467 == 8 then
						generateShapePathValue426 = -positionValue463 * 0.5 + numericValue468 * positionValue463 * 0.5
						generateShapePathValue427 = 0
					elseif numericValue467 == 9 then
						generateShapePathValue427 = numericValue468 * positionValue463 * 0.5
						generateShapePathValue426 = 0
					elseif numericValue467 == 10 then
						generateShapePathValue426 = -numericValue468 * numericValue464
						generateShapePathValue427 = positionValue463 * 0.5
					else
						generateShapePathValue426 = 0
						generateShapePathValue427 = positionValue463 * 0.5 - numericValue468 * positionValue463 * 0.5
					end

					numericValue465[#numericValue465 + 1] = Vector3.new(generateShapePathValue426, generateShapePathValue427, 0)
				end

				local positionValue469 = { 0 }
				local total2 = 0

				for i38 = 2, #numericValue465 do
					total2 = total2 + (numericValue465[i38] - numericValue465[i38 - 1]).Magnitude
					positionValue469[i38] = total2
				end

				local numericValue470 = 2

				for i39 = 0, 3000 do
					local temporaryValue471 = i39 / 3000 * total2

					while numericValue470 < #positionValue469 and positionValue469[numericValue470] < temporaryValue471 do
						numericValue470 = numericValue470 + 1
					end

					local positionValue472 = positionValue469[numericValue470 - 1]
					local positionValue473 = positionValue469[numericValue470]
					local positionValue474 = positionValue473 == positionValue472 and 0 or (temporaryValue471 - positionValue472) / (positionValue473 - positionValue472)
					sparklesTargetCache[i39] = numericValue465[numericValue470 - 1]:Lerp(numericValue465[numericValue470], positionValue474) + Vector3.new(0, 2, 0)
				end
			elseif sparklesType == "Triangle" then
				for i40 = 0, 3000 do
					local numericValue475 = i40 / 3000 * math.pi * 2
					local numericValue476 = math.floor(numericValue475 / (math.pi * 2 / 3)) % 3

					if numericValue476 == 0 then
						generateShapePathValue424 = 0
						generateShapePathValue425 = 10 * numericValue436
					elseif numericValue476 == 1 then
						generateShapePathValue424 = 8 * numericValue436
						generateShapePathValue425 = -6 * numericValue436
					else
						generateShapePathValue424 = -8 * numericValue436
						generateShapePathValue425 = -6 * numericValue436
					end

					local positionValue477 = numericValue436 * math.sin(numericValue475 * 0.5)
					sparklesTargetCache[i40] = Vector3.new(generateShapePathValue424 + positionValue477 * 0.3, generateShapePathValue425 + positionValue477 * 0.3 + 2, 0)
				end
			end

			sparklesTargetSelected = true
			return
		end
	end

	function getSmoothedShapePos(index105)
		if #sparklesTargetCache == 0 then
			return Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
		else
			local positionValue478 = index105 * #sparklesTargetCache
			local positionValue479 = math.floor(positionValue478) % #sparklesTargetCache
			return sparklesTargetCache[positionValue479]:Lerp(sparklesTargetCache[(positionValue479 + 1) % #sparklesTargetCache], positionValue478 - math.floor(positionValue478))
		end
	end

	function SE_CP()
		local part2 = Instance.new("Part")
		part2.Anchored = true
		part2.CanCollide = false
		part2.Transparency = 1
		part2.Size = Vector3.new(1, 1, 1)
		part2.Parent = workspace

		return part2
	end

	function SE_rebuildHearts()
		for index106, value282 in ipairs(sparklesConnections) do
			if value282.Handle then
				value282.Handle:Destroy()
			end
		end

		sparklesConnections = {}

		for i41 = 1, sparklesAmount do
			sparklesConnections[i41] = { Handle = SE_CP() }
		end
	end

	function SE_countAllHeartSparkler()
		local count10 = 0

		for index107, value283 in ipairs((getAllPalletFolders())) do
			for index108, value284 in ipairs(value283:GetChildren()) do
				if value284.Name == "HeartSparkler" then
					count10 = count10 + 1
				end
			end
		end

		return count10
	end

	function SE_cleanupALL()
		for index109, value285 in ipairs((getAllPalletFolders())) do
			for index110, value286 in ipairs(value285:GetChildren()) do
				local childCollection480 = value286

				if childCollection480.Name == "HeartSparkler" then
					pcall(function()
						DeleteToyRE:FireServer(childCollection480)
					end)
					sparklesObjects[childCollection480] = nil
				end
			end
		end
	end

	function SE_cleanupExcess()
		local count11 = 0

		for index111, value287 in ipairs((getAllPalletFolders())) do
			for index112, value288 in ipairs(value287:GetChildren()) do
				local childCollection481 = value288

				if childCollection481.Name == "HeartSparkler" then
					count11 = count11 + 1

					if count11 > sparklesAmount then
						pcall(function()
							DeleteToyRE:FireServer(childCollection481)
						end)
						sparklesObjects[childCollection481] = nil
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end

	function SE_CBM(p118)
		local heartGyro = p118:FindFirstChild("HeartGyro") or Instance.new("BodyGyro")
		local heartPos = p118:FindFirstChild("HeartPos") or Instance.new("BodyPosition")
		heartGyro.Name = "HeartGyro"
		heartPos.Name = "HeartPos"

		heartGyro.P = 15000
		heartGyro.D = 200

		heartPos.P = 15000
		heartPos.D = 200

		heartGyro.MaxTorque = Vector3.new(1, 1, 1) * 10000000000
		heartPos.MaxForce = Vector3.new(1, 1, 1) * 10000000000
		heartGyro.Parent = p118
		heartPos.Parent = p118
		return heartGyro, heartPos
	end

	function SE_forceNetworkOwnership(key79)
		if sparklesObjects[key79] then
			return
		end

		sparklesObjects[key79] = true

		task.spawn(function()
			local base = key79:FindFirstChild("Base")
				or key79:WaitForChild("SoundPart", 3)
				or key79.PrimaryPart
				or key79:FindFirstChildOfClass("BasePart")

			if not base then
				return
			end

			for index113, value289 in ipairs(key79:GetDescendants()) do
				if value289:IsA("BasePart") then
					value289.CanCollide = false
				end
			end

			for i42 = 1, 5 do
				SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(base, base.CFrame)
				task.wait(0.1)
			end
		end)
	end

	function SE_setupAntiGrab(p119)
		local partObject482 = tick()

		task.spawn(function()
			local base2 = p119:FindFirstChild("Base")
				or p119:WaitForChild("SoundPart", 3)
				or p119.PrimaryPart
				or p119:FindFirstChildOfClass("BasePart")

			if not base2 then
				return
			end

			task.wait(1.5)

			while p119.Parent do
				local partOwner9 = base2:FindFirstChild("PartOwner")

				if sparklesRainbowEnabled and tick() - partObject482 > 2 and (partOwner9 and partOwner9.Value ~= localPlayer.Name or not partOwner9) then
					local character81 = localPlayer.Character
					local rootPart483 = character81 and character81:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

					if rootPart483 and rootPart483.Parent then
						local cframe30 = rootPart483.CFrame
						rootPart483.CFrame = base2.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 5, 0)

						for i43 = 1, 8 do
							SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(base2, base2.CFrame)
						end

						task.wait(0.05)
						rootPart483.CFrame = cframe30
					end
				end

				task.wait(0.01)
			end
		end)
	end

	function SE_watchFolder(key80)
		if not key80 or playerDropdownValues[key80] then
			return
		else
			local getChildren2 = key80.GetChildren
			playerDropdownValues[key80] = true

			for index114, heartSparkler in ipairs(getChildren2(key80)) do
				if heartSparkler.Name == "FireworkSparkler" then
					task.wait(0.2)
					heartSparkler.Name = "HeartSparkler"
					SE_forceNetworkOwnership(heartSparkler)
					SE_setupAntiGrab(heartSparkler)
				end
			end

			key80.ChildAdded:Connect(function(heartSparkler2)
				if not sparklesEnabled then
					return
				end

				if heartSparkler2.Name == "FireworkSparkler" then
					task.wait(0.2)
					heartSparkler2.Name = "HeartSparkler"
					SE_forceNetworkOwnership(heartSparkler2)
					SE_setupAntiGrab(heartSparkler2)
				end
			end)

			return
		end
	end

	function SE_spawnSparkler(index115)
		if not canSpawn() or sparklesLoopEnabled or not sparklesEnabled then
			return
		elseif not getCurrentSpawnFolder() then
			return
		else
			local sE_spawnSparklerValue484 = sparklesAmount - SE_countAllHeartSparkler()

			if sE_spawnSparklerValue484 <= 0 then
				return
			end

			sparklesLoopEnabled = true

			for i44 = 1, sE_spawnSparklerValue484 do
				local transform485 = i44

				task.spawn(function()
					task.wait((transform485 - 1) * 0.05)
					local lookVector6 = workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame.LookVector
					local transform486 = index115.Position + lookVector6 * -1

					SpawnToyRF:InvokeServer(
						"FireworkSparkler",
						CFrame.new(transform486, transform486 + lookVector6),
						Vector3.new(0, 90, 0)
					)
				end)
			end

			task.delay(1, function()
				sparklesLoopEnabled = false
			end)
			return
		end
	end

	task.spawn(function()
		while true do
			task.wait(2)

			if sparklesEnabled then
				for index116, value290 in ipairs((getAllPalletFolders())) do
					SE_watchFolder(value290)
				end
			end
		end
	end)

	function isFoodModel(p120)
		for index123, value298 in ipairs(foodModels) do
			if p120 == value298 then
				return true
			end
		end

		return false
	end

	function smartFindHoldables()
		local childCollection500 = {}
		local count14 = 0

		for index124, value299 in ipairs(workspace:GetChildren()) do
			if count14 >= 50 then
				break
			end

			if value299:IsA("Model") and isFoodModel(value299.Name) and value299:FindFirstChild("HoldPart") then
				table.insert(childCollection500, value299)
				count14 = count14 + 1
			end

			for index125, value300 in ipairs(value299:GetChildren()) do
				if count14 >= 50 then
					break
				end

				if value300:IsA("Model") and isFoodModel(value300.Name) and value300:FindFirstChild("HoldPart") then
					table.insert(childCollection500, value300)
					count14 = count14 + 1
				end
			end
		end

		return childCollection500
	end

	function isValidTrackedObject(key81)
		if not key81 or not key81.Parent then
			return false
		end

		local holdItemRemoteFunction4, use

		if failedObjects[key81] and tick() - failedObjects[key81] < 5 then
			return false
		else
			local holdPart4 = key81:FindFirstChild("HoldPart")

			if not holdPart4 then
				return false
			end

			holdItemRemoteFunction4 = holdPart4:FindFirstChild("HoldItemRemoteFunction")

			if holdItemRemoteFunction4 then
				if not pcall(function()
					holdItemRemoteFunction4:InvokeServer(key81, localPlayer.Character)
				end) then
					failedObjects[key81] = tick()
					return false
				else
					task.wait(0.03)
					local holdEvents = replicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("HoldEvents")

					if holdEvents then
						use = holdEvents:FindFirstChild("Use")

						if use then
							pcall(function()
								use:FireServer(key81)
							end)
						end
					end

					heldObjects[key81] = tick()
					failedObjects[key81] = nil
					return true
				end
			else
				return false
			end
		end
	end

	function dropToVoid(key82)
		local transform501 = not key82 or not key82.Parent
		local cframe32, dropItemRemoteFunction3

		if transform501 then
			heldObjects[key82] = nil
			return
		else
			local holdPart5 = key82:FindFirstChild("HoldPart")

			if not holdPart5 then
				heldObjects[key82] = nil
				return
			end

			cframe32 = CFrame.new(0, -500, 0)
			dropItemRemoteFunction3 = holdPart5:FindFirstChild("DropItemRemoteFunction")

			if dropItemRemoteFunction3 then
				pcall(function()
					dropItemRemoteFunction3:InvokeServer(key82, cframe32, Vector3.new(0, 97.69, 0))
				end)
			end

			heldObjects[key82] = nil
			return
		end
	end

	lastTick = 0
	lastCleanup = 0

	function auraLoop()
		while auraActive do
			local serviceObject502 = tick()

			if serviceObject502 - lastTick < 0.4 then
				runService.Heartbeat:Wait()
			else
				lastTick = serviceObject502

				if serviceObject502 - lastCleanup > 3 then
					for key83, value301 in pairs(heldObjects) do
						if not key83 or not key83.Parent then
							heldObjects[key83] = nil
						end
					end

					for key84, value302 in pairs(failedObjects) do
						if not key84 or not key84.Parent or serviceObject502 - value302 > 10 then
							failedObjects[key84] = nil
						end
					end

					lastCleanup = serviceObject502
				end

				local count15 = 0
				local count16 = 0

				for index126, value303 in ipairs((smartFindHoldables())) do
					if count15 >= 10 or count16 >= 20 then
						break
					end

					count16 = count16 + 1

					if value303.Parent and not heldObjects[value303] then
						if isValidTrackedObject(value303) then
							count15 = count15 + 1
						end
					end
				end

				local count17 = 0

				for key85, value304 in pairs(heldObjects) do
					if count17 >= 5 then
						break
					end

					if key85.Parent and serviceObject502 - value304 >= 2.7 then
						dropToVoid(key85)
						count17 = count17 + 1
					elseif not key85.Parent then
						heldObjects[key85] = nil
					end
				end

				runService.Heartbeat:Wait()
			end
		end
	end

				failedObjects = {}
				lastTick = 0
				lastCleanup = 0

			end
		end,
	})

	function isInstrumentModel(p121)
		for index127, value306 in ipairs(instrumentModels) do
			if p121 == value306 then
				return true
			end
		end

		return false
	end

	function smartFindInstruments()
		local childCollection503 = {}
		local count18 = 0

		for index128, value307 in ipairs(workspace:GetChildren()) do
			if count18 >= 50 then
				break
			end

			if value307:IsA("Model") and isInstrumentModel(value307.Name) and value307:FindFirstChild("HoldPart") then
				table.insert(childCollection503, value307)
				count18 = count18 + 1
			end

			for index129, value308 in ipairs(value307:GetChildren()) do
				if count18 >= 50 then
					break
				end

				if
					value308:IsA("Model")
					and isInstrumentModel(value308.Name)
					and value308:FindFirstChild("HoldPart")
				then
					table.insert(childCollection503, value308)
					count18 = count18 + 1
				end
			end
		end

		return childCollection503
	end

	function isValidOwnedObject(key86)
		if not key86 or not key86.Parent then
			return false
		end

		local holdItemRemoteFunction5, use2

		if instrumentFailedObjects[key86] and tick() - instrumentFailedObjects[key86] < 5 then
			return false
		else
			local holdPart6 = key86:FindFirstChild("HoldPart")

			if not holdPart6 then
				return false
			end

			holdItemRemoteFunction5 = holdPart6:FindFirstChild("HoldItemRemoteFunction")

			if holdItemRemoteFunction5 then
				if not pcall(function()
					holdItemRemoteFunction5:InvokeServer(key86, localPlayer.Character)
				end) then
					instrumentFailedObjects[key86] = tick()
					return false
				else
					task.wait(0.03)
					local holdEvents2 = replicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("HoldEvents")

					if holdEvents2 then
						use2 = holdEvents2:FindFirstChild("Use")

						if use2 then
							pcall(function()
								use2:FireServer(key86)
							end)
						end
					end

					instrumentHeldObjects[key86] = tick()
					instrumentFailedObjects[key86] = nil
					return true
				end
			else
				return false
			end
		end
	end

	function dropInstrumentToVoid(key87)
		local transform504 = not key87 or not key87.Parent
		local cframe33, transform505

		if transform504 then
			instrumentHeldObjects[key87] = nil
			return
		else
			local holdPart7 = key87:FindFirstChild("HoldPart")

			if not holdPart7 then
				instrumentHeldObjects[key87] = nil
				return
			else
				local findFirstChild31 = holdPart7.FindFirstChild
				cframe33 = CFrame.new(0, -500, 0)
				transform505 = findFirstChild31(holdPart7, "DropItemRemoteFunction")

				if transform505 then
					pcall(function()
						transform505:InvokeServer(key87, cframe33, Vector3.new(0, 97.69, 0))
					end)
				end

				instrumentHeldObjects[key87] = nil
				return
			end
		end
	end

	instrumentLastTick = 0
	instrumentLastCleanup = 0

	function instrumentAuraLoop()
		while instrumentAuraActive do
			local serviceObject506 = tick()

			if serviceObject506 - instrumentLastTick < 0.4 then
				runService.Heartbeat:Wait()
			else
				instrumentLastTick = serviceObject506

				if serviceObject506 - instrumentLastCleanup > 3 then
					for key88, value309 in pairs(instrumentHeldObjects) do
						if not key88 or not key88.Parent then
							instrumentHeldObjects[key88] = nil
						end
					end

					for key89, value310 in pairs(instrumentFailedObjects) do
						if not key89 or not key89.Parent or serviceObject506 - value310 > 10 then
							instrumentFailedObjects[key89] = nil
						end
					end

					instrumentLastCleanup = serviceObject506
				end

				local count19 = 0
				local count20 = 0

				for index130, value311 in ipairs((smartFindInstruments())) do
					if count19 >= 10 or count20 >= 20 then
						break
					end

					count20 = count20 + 1

					if value311.Parent and not instrumentHeldObjects[value311] then
						if isValidOwnedObject(value311) then
							count19 = count19 + 1
						end
					end
				end

				local count21 = 0

				for key90, value312 in pairs(instrumentHeldObjects) do
					if count21 >= 5 then
						break
					end

					if key90.Parent and serviceObject506 - value312 >= 2.7 then
						dropInstrumentToVoid(key90)
						count21 = count21 + 1
					elseif not key90.Parent then
						instrumentHeldObjects[key90] = nil
					end
				end

				runService.Heartbeat:Wait()
			end
		end
	end

	})

	function toy_spawn(p122, index131, p123)
		local transform507 = typeof(p123) == "string"
				and string.lower(p123) == "headup"
				and CFrame.new(index131.Position + Vector3.new(0, 1, 0), index131.Position) * CFrame.Angles(
					math.pi,
					0,
					0
				)
			or index131

		task.spawn(function()
			SpawnToyRF:InvokeServer(p122, transform507, typeof(p123) == "Vector3" and p123 or Vector3.zero)
		end)

		local childCollection508 = tick()
		local childCollection509

		local connect39 = BackPack.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child16)
			if child16.Name == p122 then
				childCollection509 = child16
			end
		end)

		if not childCollection509 then
			tick()
		end

		local timestamp510

		repeat
			task.wait()
			timestamp510 = childCollection509 or tick() - childCollection508 > 5
		until timestamp510

		connect39:Disconnect()
		return childCollection509
	end

	function UpdateRagdollPalletMenu()
		task.spawn(function()
			local playerGui2 = player:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")

			if not playerGui2 then
				return
			else
				local menuGui2 = playerGui2:FindFirstChild("MenuGui")

				if not menuGui2 or not scriptRagdollToy or not scriptRagdollToy:FindFirstChild("ScriptRagdollID") then
					return
				else
					local contents2 = menuGui2.Menu.TabContents.ToyDestroy.Contents
					local value314 = scriptRagdollToy.ScriptRagdollID.Value
					local childCollection511 = {}

					for index132, value315 in ipairs(BackPack:GetChildren()) do
						if value315.Name == "RagdollPallet" or value315.Name == "PalletLightBrown" then
							table.insert(childCollection511, value315)
						end
					end

					local foundInstance512 = 0

					for index133, value316 in ipairs(childCollection511) do
						if
							value316:FindFirstChild("ScriptRagdollID")
							and value316.ScriptRagdollID.Value == value314
						then
							foundInstance512 = index133
							break
						end
					end

					if foundInstance512 == 0 then
						return
					else
						local count22 = 0

						for index134, value317 in ipairs(contents2:GetChildren()) do
							if value317.Name == "PalletLightBrown" and value317:FindFirstChild("ViewItemButton") then
								count22 = count22 + 1

								if count22 == foundInstance512 then
									local viewItemButton2 = value317.ViewItemButton
									viewItemButton2.Text = "Ragdoll Controller (DO NOT DELETE)"
									viewItemButton2.TextScaled = true

									if viewItemButton2:FindFirstChild("LowResImage") then
										viewItemButton2.LowResImage.Image = ""
									end

									return
								end
							end
						end

						return
					end
				end
			end
		end)
	end

	function runBodyForm()
		if not formEnabled or scriptRagdollToy or isRespawning then
			return
		else
			isRespawning = true
			local generateGUID2 = HttpService:GenerateGUID(false)

			for i45 = 1, 5 do
				local head16 = player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChild("Head")

				if not head16 then
					isRespawning = false
					return
				else
					local ragdollPallet = toy_spawn("PalletLightBrown", head16, "headup")

					if ragdollPallet then
						local scriptRagdollID2 = Instance.new("StringValue")
						scriptRagdollID2.Name = "ScriptRagdollID"
						scriptRagdollID2.Value = generateGUID2
						scriptRagdollID2.Parent = ragdollPallet

						ragdollPallet.Name = "RagdollPallet"
						local waitForChild15 = ragdollPallet:WaitForChild("SoundPart", 5)

						if waitForChild15 then
							task.spawn(function()
								while formEnabled and waitForChild15 and waitForChild15.Parent do
									if
										not waitForChild15:FindFirstChild("PartOwner")
										or waitForChild15.PartOwner.Value ~= player.Name
									then
										SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(waitForChild15, waitForChild15.CFrame)
									end

									task.wait(0.05)
								end
							end)

							local instance5 = Instance.new("BodyVelocity", waitForChild15)
							instance5.MaxForce = Vector3.new(0, math.huge, 0)
							instance5.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, 1000, 0)

							for key91, value318 in pairs(ragdollPallet:GetChildren()) do
								if value318:IsA("BasePart") then
									value318.Transparency = 1
									value318.Size = Vector3.new(0.5, 0.5, 0.5)
									value318.CanCollide = false
								end
							end

							if waitForChild15:FindFirstChild("Wood") then
								waitForChild15.Wood:Destroy()
							end

							scriptRagdollToy = ragdollPallet
							RAGDOLL_UNIQUE_ID = generateGUID2
							isRespawning = false
							UpdateRagdollPalletMenu()
							return
						end
					end

					task.wait(0.3)
				end
			end

			isRespawning = false
			return
		end
	end

	function DestroyRagdoll()
		if scriptRagdollToy and scriptRagdollToy.Parent then
			pcall(function()
				DestroyToyRF:FireServer(scriptRagdollToy)
			end)
			task.wait(0.1)
		end

		scriptRagdollToy = nil
	end

	function StartRagdollMonitor()
		if ragdollMonitor then
			task.cancel(ragdollMonitor)
		end

		ragdollMonitor = task.spawn(function()
			while formEnabled do
				task.wait(1)

				if
					not scriptRagdollToy
					or not scriptRagdollToy.Parent
					or not scriptRagdollToy:FindFirstChild("ScriptRagdollID")
					or scriptRagdollToy.ScriptRagdollID.Value ~= RAGDOLL_UNIQUE_ID
				then
					scriptRagdollToy = nil
					runBodyForm()
				end
			end
		end)
	end

	BackPack.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child17)
		if not formEnabled then
			return
		end

		task.wait(0.05)

		if
			child17:FindFirstChild("ScriptRagdollID")
			and child17.ScriptRagdollID.Value == RAGDOLL_UNIQUE_ID
			and child17.Parent
		then
			scriptRagdollToy = child17
			UpdateRagdollPalletMenu()
		end
	end)

	BackPack.ChildRemoved:Connect(function(child18)
		if formEnabled and child18 == scriptRagdollToy then
			scriptRagdollToy = nil
			task.wait(0.5)

			if formEnabled and not scriptRagdollToy then
				runBodyForm()
			end
		end
	end)

	function GetGrabbedCharacter()
		local grabParts10 = workspace:FindFirstChild("GrabParts")

		if not grabParts10 then
			return nil
		else
			local grabPart11 = grabParts10:FindFirstChild("GrabPart")

			if not grabPart11 then
				return nil
			else
				local weldConstraint10 = grabPart11:FindFirstChildOfClass("WeldConstraint")
					or grabPart11:FindFirstChild("Weld")

				return weldConstraint10
					and weldConstraint10.Part1
					and weldConstraint10.Part1:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
			end
		end
	end

	function RestoreCollisions(p124)
		if not p124 then
			return
		end

		if p124.torso and p124.torso.Parent then
			p124.torso.CanCollide = true
		end

		if p124.head and p124.head.Parent then
			p124.head.CanCollide = true
		end

		if p124.hrp and p124.hrp.Parent then
			p124.hrp.CanCollide = true
		end

		for index135, value319 in ipairs(p124.limbs) do
			if value319.part and value319.part.Parent then
				value319.part.CanCollide = true
			end
		end
	end

	function ApplyNoclipToForm(p125)
		if p125.torso and p125.torso.Parent then
			p125.torso.CanCollide = false
		end

		if p125.head and p125.head.Parent then
			p125.head.CanCollide = false
		end

		if p125.hrp and p125.hrp.Parent then
			p125.hrp.CanCollide = false
		end

		for index136, value320 in ipairs(p125.limbs) do
			if value320.part and value320.part.Parent then
				value320.part.CanCollide = false
			end
		end
	end

	function GetCharacterParts(p126)
		if not p126 then
			return nil
		else
			local torso6 = p126:FindFirstChild("Torso")

			if not torso6 then
				return nil
			else
				local foundInstance513 = {}

				for index137, value321 in ipairs({ "Left Arm", "Right Arm", "Left Leg", "Right Leg" }) do
					local findFirstChild32 = p126:FindFirstChild(value321)

					if findFirstChild32 then
						local ragdollLimbPart = findFirstChild32:FindFirstChild("RagdollLimbPart")

						if ragdollLimbPart and ragdollLimbPart:IsA("BasePart") then
							table.insert(foundInstance513, { part = ragdollLimbPart, name = value321 })
						end
					end
				end

				return {
					torso = torso6,
					head = p126:FindFirstChild("Head"),
					hrp = p126:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"),
					limbs = foundInstance513,
				}
			end
		end
	end

	function initializeBodyForm()
		local character85 = player.Character

		if not character85 then
			return
		else
			local torso7 = character85:FindFirstChild("Torso")
				or character85:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
				or character85:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

			if not torso7 then
				return
			else
				local parts = activeForm.parts

				if parts.hrp and parts.hrp.Parent then
					parts.hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(0, 0, 0)
					parts.hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
					parts.hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
				end

				local unit3 = Vector3.new(torso7.CFrame.LookVector.X, 0, torso7.CFrame.LookVector.Z).Unit

				local transform514 = Vector3.new(torso7.Position.X, torso7.Position.Y, torso7.Position.Z) + unit3 * formDistance

				local transform515 = CFrame.new(transform514, transform514 + unit3) * CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(formOrientation), 0)

				ApplyNoclipToForm(parts)

				parts.torso.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
				parts.torso.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
				parts.torso.Anchored = false
				parts.torso.CFrame = transform515

				if parts.head and parts.head.Parent then
					local head17 = character85:FindFirstChild("Head")

					if head17 then
						parts.head.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
						parts.head.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
						parts.head.Anchored = false

						local cframe34 = torso7.CFrame
						parts.head.CFrame = transform515 * cframe34:ToObjectSpace(head17.CFrame)
					end
				end

				limbMap = {
					["Left Arm"] = character85:FindFirstChild("Left Arm") or character85:FindFirstChild("LeftUpperArm"),
					["Right Arm"] = character85:FindFirstChild("Right Arm")
						or character85:FindFirstChild("RightUpperArm"),
					["Left Leg"] = character85:FindFirstChild("Left Leg") or character85:FindFirstChild("LeftUpperLeg"),
					["Right Leg"] = character85:FindFirstChild("Right Leg")
						or character85:FindFirstChild("RightUpperLeg"),
				}

				for index138, value322 in ipairs(parts.limbs) do
					local positionValue516 = limbMap[value322.name]

					if value322.part and value322.part.Parent and positionValue516 then
						value322.part.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
						value322.part.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
						value322.part.Anchored = false

						local toObjectSpace = torso7.CFrame:ToObjectSpace(positionValue516.CFrame)

						value322.part.CFrame = transform515
							* toObjectSpace
							* CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(90), math.rad(270))
							* CFrame.new(0.4, 0, 0)
					end
				end

				return
			end
		end
	end

	function ApplyForm()
		if not activeForm or not formEnabled then
			return
		else
			local head18 = activeForm.char:FindFirstChild("Head")

			if not head18 or not head18:FindFirstChild("PartOwner") then
				return
			elseif copyMovementEnabled then
				initializeBodyForm()
				return
			else
				local parts2 = activeForm.parts
				local character86 = player.Character

				if not character86 then
					return
				else
					local torso8 = character86:FindFirstChild("Torso") or character86:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

					if not torso8 or not parts2.torso or not parts2.torso.Parent then
						return
					else
						if parts2.hrp and parts2.hrp.Parent then
							parts2.hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(0, 0, 0)
							parts2.hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
							parts2.hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
						end

						local unit4 = Vector3.new(torso8.CFrame.LookVector.X, 0, torso8.CFrame.LookVector.Z).Unit

						local transform517 = Vector3.new(torso8.Position.X, torso8.Position.Y, torso8.Position.Z)
							+ unit4 * formDistance

						local transform518 = CFrame.new(transform517, transform517 + unit4) * CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(formOrientation), 0)

						local transform519 = formPresets[currentPreset]
						ApplyNoclipToForm(parts2)

						parts2.torso.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
						parts2.torso.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
						parts2.torso.Anchored = false
						parts2.torso.CFrame = transform518 * CFrame.new(transform519.Torso.pos) * transform519.Torso.rot

						if parts2.head and parts2.head.Parent then
							parts2.head.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
							parts2.head.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
							parts2.head.Anchored = false
							parts2.head.CFrame = transform518 * CFrame.new(transform519.Head.pos) * transform519.Head.rot
						end

						for index139, value323 in ipairs(parts2.limbs) do
							if value323.part and value323.part.Parent then
								value323.part.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
								value323.part.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
								value323.part.Anchored = false

								local transform520 = transform519[value323.name]

								if transform520 then
									value323.part.CFrame = transform518 * CFrame.new(transform520.pos) * transform520.rot
								end
							end
						end

						return
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end

	function SetupForm(p127)
		if not formEnabled then
			return
		end

		local humanoid521 = p127:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

		if not humanoid521 then
			return
		else
			task.wait(0.3)
			local character522 = GetCharacterParts(p127)

			if not character522 then
				return
			end

			activeForm = {
				char = p127,
				parts = character522,
				isGrabbed = true,
				connection = nil,
			}

			task.spawn(function()
				while activeForm and activeForm.char == p127 and p127.Parent and formEnabled do
					local head19 = p127:FindFirstChild("Head")

					if
						not (head19 and head19:FindFirstChild("PartOwner") and head19.PartOwner.Value == player.Name)
					then
						local rootPart523 = player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

						if head19 and rootPart523 and (rootPart523.Position - head19.Position).Magnitude <= 60 then
							SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(head19, head19.CFrame)
						end
					end

					local ragdolled4 = humanoid521:FindFirstChild("Ragdolled")

					if ragdolled4 and not ragdolled4.Value and scriptRagdollToy then
						local soundPart = scriptRagdollToy:FindFirstChild("SoundPart")

						if soundPart and head19 then
							firetouchinterest(soundPart, head19, 0)
							task.wait()
							firetouchinterest(soundPart, head19, 1)
						end
					end

					task.wait(0.1)
				end
			end)

			activeForm.connection = runService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
				if not formEnabled then
					return
				end

				activeForm.isGrabbed = GetGrabbedCharacter() == p127
				ApplyForm()
			end)

			return
		end
	end

	function StopCurrentForm()
		if activeForm then
			if activeForm.parts then
				RestoreCollisions(activeForm.parts)
			end

			if activeForm.connection then
				activeForm.connection:Disconnect()
			end
		end

		activeForm = nil
	end

	FormTab:CreateLabel("Form Grab", "Hand")

	FormTab:CreateToggle({
		Name = 'Enable Form Grab   <font face="GothamBlack" color="rgb(240,184,111)">PALLET</font>   <font face="GothamBlack" color="rgb(0,140,255)">RAGDOLL</font>',
		CurrentValue = false,
		Flag = "FormGrabToggle",
		Callback = function(value324)
			formEnabled = value324

			if value324 then
				runBodyForm()
				StartRagdollMonitor()
			else
				StopCurrentForm()
				DestroyRagdoll()

				if ragdollMonitor then
					task.cancel(ragdollMonitor)
					ragdollMonitor = nil
				end
			end
		end,
	})

	FormTab:CreateSlider({
		Name = 'Distance   <font face="GothamBlack" color="rgb(0,184,111)">STUDS</font>',
		Range = { 0, 20 },
		Increment = 1,
		CurrentValue = 10,
		Flag = "FormDistance",
		Callback = function(value325)
			formDistance = value325
		end,
	})

	FormTab:CreateSlider({
		Name = 'Orientation\t  <font face="GothamBlack" color="rgb(0,255,15)">ROTATION</font>',
		Range = { 0, 360 },
		Increment = 5,
		CurrentValue = 0,
		Flag = "FormOrientation",
		Callback = function(value326)
			formOrientation = value326
		end,
	})

	FormTab:CreateDropdown({
		Name = 'Form   <font face="GothamBlack" color="rgb(0,59,255)">DROPTOWN</font>',
		Options = { "Penis Head", "L Shape", "Ice Cream", "Dog", "Square" },
		CurrentOption = { "Penis Head" },
		Flag = "FormPreset",
		Callback = function(value327)
			if copyMovementEnabled then
				return
			end

			currentPreset = value327[1]
		end,
	})

	FormTab:CreateToggle({
		Name = 'Copy Movement\t<font face="GothamBlack" color="rgb(240,140,0)">TUFF</font>\t  <font face="GothamBlack" color="rgb(39,245,218)">PREMIUM</font>',
		CurrentValue = false,
		Flag = "CopyMovement",
		Callback = function(value328)
			copyMovementEnabled = value328
		end,
	})

	FormTab:CreateButton({
		Name = 'Drop Current Target\t  <font face="GothamBlack" color="rgb(255,5,8)">STOP</font>',
		Callback = function()
			StopCurrentForm()
		end,
	})

	FormTab:CreateParagraph({
		Title = "NOTE",
		Content = 'DO   <font face="GothamBlack" color="rgb(255,0,0)">NOT ENABLE</font>   Form Grab in House.  Form Grab Work on <font face="GothamBlack" color="rgb(255,0,0)">NPC</font> and <font face="GothamBlack" color="rgb(255,0,0)">PLAYER</font>',
	})

	coroutine.wrap(function()
		while task.wait(0.1) do
			if formEnabled then
				pcall(function()
					local character524 = GetGrabbedCharacter()

					if character524 and not activeForm then
						SetupForm(character524)
					end

					if activeForm and (not activeForm.char or not activeForm.char.Parent) then
						rayfield:Notify({
							Title = "ERROR",
							Image = "x",
							Content = "Target Lost",
							Duration = 3,
						})

						StopCurrentForm()
						return
					end

					if activeForm and activeForm.char then
						local character87 = player.Character
						local head20 = activeForm.char:FindFirstChild("Head")

						if character87 and head20 then
							local rootPart525 = character87:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

							if rootPart525 then
								if (rootPart525.Position - head20.Position).Magnitude > 30 then
									rayfield:Notify({
										Title = "ERROR",
										Image = "x",
										Content = "Target exceeded 30 studs - Drop",
										Duration = 3,
									})

									StopCurrentForm()
									return
								end
							end
						end
					end

					if activeForm and activeForm.char then
						local getPlayerFromCharacter2 = players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(activeForm.char)

						if getPlayerFromCharacter2 then
							local findFirstChild33 = workspace:FindFirstChild("Players")
								and workspace.Players:FindFirstChild(getPlayerFromCharacter2.Name)

							if findFirstChild33 then
								local inPlot2 = findFirstChild33:FindFirstChild("InPlot")

								if inPlot2 and inPlot2.Value == true then
									rayfield:Notify({
										Title = "ERROR",
										Image = "x",
										Content = "Target in plot - Drop",
										Duration = 3,
									})

									StopCurrentForm()
									return
								end
							end

							local inPlot3 = getPlayerFromCharacter2:FindFirstChild("InPlot")

							if inPlot3 and inPlot3.Value == true then
								rayfield:Notify({
									Title = "ERROR",
									Image = "x",
									Content = "Target in plot - Drop",
									Duration = 3,
								})

								StopCurrentForm()
								return
							end

							return
						end

						return
					end
				end)
			end
		end
	end)()

	player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
		if activeForm and activeForm.connection then
			activeForm.connection:Disconnect()
		end

		activeForm = nil
	end)

	notify("Bloody [PREMIUM]")
	return
end
